Abstract: Many text classification decisions are viable based on constituent excerpts alone. Taking inspiration from the field of multiple instance learning, we present an algorithm for training a neural network to classify text by selecting such excerpts. We show that our approach is also scalable with demonstrated learning against samples with nearly 1M tokens. We evaluate our methods on 7 datasets with emphasis on long-textual collections that far exceed the encoding limit of our base model. We present state-of-the-art results with this algorithm on 3 datasets: identification of political bias in news outlets, trigger warnings in long stories, and demographic characteristics of authors in tweet collections. Furthermore, the model trained on weakly-labeled collections of text (bags) generalizes to accurately classify constituent, smaller instances. Besides a new state-of-the-art for these problems, this approach is one of the few neural methods to excel in these datasets.

</details>


### [42] [Directional Contextual Representations for Dependency Relations: Why Cross-Direction Pairing Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20647)
*Sai Krishna Arthanari,JaeHyeong Chang,Chengzhe Sun,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究双向LSTM的表示用于依存关系类型分类。发现将前向状态F_i和后向状态B_i拆分（同方向配对）优于融合表示或任一单独方向；但"跨方向配对"（F_i对B_j）一致性地逊于同方向配对，且此劣势随token距离增大而增大。通过冻结主干（frozen-trunk）方法学诊断，排除架构信息泄漏和训练共适应两类解释，并通过线性回归、线性探针和多个冻结主干探针，表明表示冗余和前瞻编码只构成部分原因，机制根源在于方向信息存储但定位不精确、且仅传播少数token即衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双向LSTM中前向与后向表示的交互机制，尤其是为何一种看似自然的"跨方向配对"扩展会在依存关系类型分类中持续表现不佳，并随距离增大而恶化，从而揭示双向下文编码的底层特征。

Method: 采用冻结主干（frozen-trunk）方法学严格诊断归因：单层BiLSTM代码检查排除架构泄漏；冻结主干仅训练新头部，排除训练共适应；线性回归衡量F_i与B_i的表示冗余（R²对比打乱控制）；线性探针检测F_i对后续token的前瞻编码；扩展位置探针和距离衰减探针检验方向信息的定位精度与传播范围；配对的bootstrap显著性检验验证性能差异。

Result: 跨方向配对一致地劣于同方向配对，且惩罚随距离增长（经配对bootstrap检验显著）。93%的同-跨差距在冻结主干后仍存在，排除共适应主因。F_i与B_i间存在部分冗余（R²=0.324 vs 0.028控制）且F_i含部分前瞻编码（36.5% vs 17.2%多数基线），但两者单独或联合均不能完全解释差距。位置与距离探针显示方向信息存储但定位不精确，且传播数token后即衰减到基线。

Conclusion: 双向LSTM的同方向配对胜过跨方向配对这一现象是多因而非单因的：表示冗余与前瞻编码有真实但有限的贡献，而根本机制在于方向信息存储位置模糊且衰减迅速，这从机理上支撑了距离-惩罚增长的外在表现。该结论提示跨方向特征组合并非有效的架构扩展方向。

Abstract: Splitting a bidirectional LSTM's contextual representation into a forward-only $F_i$ (strictly a function of tokens $1..i$) and a backward-only $B_i$ (strictly a function of tokens $i..n$) beats either alone and beats a fused self-attention representation for dependency relation-type classification. But a specific, natural extension of this idea -- pairing a token's forward state against a \emph{candidate}'s backward state (``cross-direction'' pairing, $F_i$ vs.\ $B_j$) -- consistently \emph{underperforms} same-direction pairing, and the penalty \emph{grows}, not shrinks, with token distance, both paired-bootstrap significant. We diagnose why using a frozen-trunk methodology: architectural information leakage between directions is impossible by construction (a single-layer BiLSTM, verified by code inspection); 93\% of the same-vs-cross gap survives freezing the trunk and training only fresh heads, ruling out training-co-adaptation as the primary cause; linear regression shows partial representational redundancy between $F_i$ and $B_i$ ($R^2{=}0.324$ vs.\ $0.028$ for a shuffled control) and a linear probe shows partial anticipatory encoding of upcoming tokens in $F_i$ (36.5\% vs.\ 17.2\% majority baseline) -- real effects, but neither alone, nor combined, cleanly explains the full gap. Extended frozen-trunk diagnostics (a positional probe and a distance-decay probe) show directional information is genuinely stored but not exactly positioned, and propagates only a few tokens before decaying to baseline -- consistent with, and mechanistically underneath, the distance-growth finding.

</details>


### [43] [AsmEvo: Agentic Assembly-Level Optimization of AMD GPU Kernels with Functional Equivalence Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20711)
*Ji Liu,Puyuan Yang,Rongzhang Zheng,Fan Wang,Jinglin Wang,Muhammad A. Awad,Mortis Huang,Andy Chang,Zekai Li,Zeping Li,Zihao An,Yue Liu,Yuchen Yang,Jianghui Wang,Chushi Chen,Ziqiong Liu,Fuwei Yang,Dong Li,Wen Heng Chung,Shengcai Liu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AsmEvo是一个针对AMD GPU已编译代码对象（AMDGPU code object）的汇编级智能优化器。它直接在最终二进制层面进行低层级编辑优化，无需源代码，通过重新构建ABI兼容的优化对象，并利用差分验证确保功能等价性，在多种AMD GPU工作负载上实现了1.09x至1.35x的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能机器学习系统依赖的GPU内核通常无法获得可编辑的源代码，现有LLM内核优化器和自动调优器主要操作CUDA、Triton等高级源代码层面，无法优化已编译的二进制对象。论文研究一个更严格的设定：在只有部署二进制作为唯一行为参考的情况下，直接优化已编译的AMDGPU代码对象。

Method: AsmEvo包含五个关键组件：1) 代码对象恢复（code-object recovery），重构可重汇编的表示；2) 元数据感知的重建（metadata-aware rebuilding），生成ABI保留的优化对象；3) 性能分析引导的热窗口编辑（profiling-guided hot-window editing），通过长时程智能体提出低层级编辑；4) 正确性门控的计时（correctness-gated timing），通过差分验证和计时筛选候选；5) 保守的就地补丁回退（conservative in-place patch fallback），确保总能生成可部署的优化对象。

Result: 在MI308X上，AsmEvo优化了30个KernelBench内核中的29个，达到1.35x几何平均和3.88x最大加速。在MI300X生产负载上，优化了所有评估的AITer二进制和vLLM/SGLang Triton汇编内核，分别达到1.09x/1.31x和1.18x/1.34x的几何平均/最大加速，同时保持功能等价性。

Conclusion: AsmEvo证明了在无源代码条件下直接优化已编译的GPU二进制对象是可行且有效的，为无法从源码层面优化的生产级GPU内核提供了新的优化途径，展示了汇编级智能优化在AMD GPU生态中的实用价值。

Abstract: High-performance ML systems increasingly rely on GPU kernels whose editable source is unavailable, generated, or too distant from final machine code to expose remaining optimizations. Existing LLM kernel optimizers and autotuners mainly operate on CUDA, Triton, HIP, or tensor-program source and validate against reference implementations. We study a stricter setting: optimizing an already compiled AMDGPU code object, where the deployed binary is the only behavioral oracle.
  We present AsmEvo, an agentic assembly-level optimizer for AMD GPU kernels. Given an AMDGPU code object K0, AsmEvo reconstructs a reassemblable representation, proposes low-level edits with a long-horizon agent, rebuilds an ABI-preserving optimized object, and accepts candidates only after differential verification against K0 under identical launches. AsmEvo combines code-object recovery, metadata-aware rebuilding, profiling-guided hot-window editing, correctness-gated timing, and conservative in-place patch fallback.
  We conduct extensive experiments with AsmEvo on various AMD GPU kernels. On MI308X, AsmEvo improves 29 of 30 selected KernelBench kernels, reaching 1.35x geometric-mean and 3.88x maximum speedup. On MI300X production workloads, it improves all evaluated AITer binaries and vLLM/SGLang Triton assembly kernels, reaching 1.09x/1.31x and 1.18x/1.34x geometric-mean/maximum speedups, respectively, while preserving functional equivalence.

</details>


### [44] [PSK at WMT 2026 MIST: Task-Specialized QLoRA Adapters for Multilingual Summarization and Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20757)
*Srikar Kashyap Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PSK系统在WMT 2026多语言指令共享任务上的提交方案，该系统基于3.35B参数的Tiny Aya Global模型，为每个任务分别训练了三个QLoRA适配器，并提交了三个使用不同开放问答适配器的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言指令任务中的不同子任务（摘要、问答、总结）具有不同的特征，单个多任务适配器可能无法在各任务上均取得最佳性能，因此需要为每个任务定制专用适配器并进行对比实验。

Method: 使用3.35B参数的Tiny Aya Global作为基座模型，为每个任务分别训练一个QLoRA适配器：多语言文档-摘要对上的摘要适配器、基于篇章的问答适配器、以及过滤后的独立问答适配器。摘要数据中还包含科学论文及其作者撰写的摘要。最终提交三个系统，共享上下文和摘要适配器，但使用不同的开放问答适配器。

Result: 在留出集上，上下文和摘要适配器的表现优于仅使用组织者提供数据训练的多任务适配器。开放问答的结果好坏参半，且随答案长度和评估方法的不同而变化。

Conclusion: 针对每个子任务使用独立的专用适配器优于多任务适配器，尤其体现在上下文和摘要任务上；由于开放问答在不同评估条件下表现不稳定，提交多个采用不同适配器的系统以覆盖不同评估偏好。

Abstract: We describe the PSK submission to the WMT 2026 Multilingual Instruction Shared Task. Our system uses the 3.35B-parameter Tiny Aya Global model with three QLoRA adapters, one for each task. The adapters are trained on multilingual document-summary pairs, passage-based question answering, and filtered standalone question answering. The summarization data also includes scientific papers with their author-written abstracts. On our held-out split, the context and summarization adapters perform better than our multitask adapter, which was trained only on data supplied by the organizers. Results for open QA are mixed and vary with answer length and evaluation method. We therefore submit three systems with the same context and summarization adapters but different open-QA adapters.

</details>


### [45] [Tree-of-Concerns: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Debate for Unstated-Limitation Extraction in Scientific Critique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20777)
*Sahil Mishra,Niranjan Rajeev,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Tree-of-Concerns多智能体框架，利用专业化怀疑论者角色和并行辩论树，系统性地从科学论文中挖掘未明说的局限性，配合Panel Review机制校正分类漂移和严重性误校准，在ToC-Bench基准上相比最强基线提升79%精确率和11%覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献规模不断增长，而论文对局限性的报告日益不足，这些隐藏的失败模式难以被系统性地识别和评估。

Method: 采用多智能体框架Tree-of-Concerns：每个专业化怀疑论者角色通过特定类别的分析视角进行证据支撑的结构化论证，形成并行辩论树；随后通过Panel Review机制从五个视角重新评估每个幸存主张，以纠正类别漂移和严重性误校准。

Result: 在ToC-Bench基准（包含414篇论文、1905个未明说局限性）上，ToC相比最强基线将精确率提升79%，覆盖率提升11%，能够提取出具体的、有证据支撑的关注点，支持审稿人的系统性评估。

Conclusion: ToC框架通过多视角辩论和面板评审的结合，显著提升了未明说局限性的识别精度与覆盖广度，为科学论文的客观评审提供了自动化辅助工具。

Abstract: As scientific literature grows and papers increasingly under-report limitations, multi-agent LLMs offer a promising approach to systematically uncover these hidden failure modes. Here, we introduce Tree-of-Concerns, a multi-agent framework that deploys specialized skeptic personas, each operating through a category-specific analytical lens, as parallel debate trees to extract unstated limitations from scientific papers. Each persona conducts structured, evidence-grounded argumentation, while a Panel Review mechanism re-evaluates each surviving claim from all five perspectives to correct category drift and severity miscalibration. Through experiments on ToC-Bench, our benchmark of 414 research papers with 1,905 unstated limitations, sourced from reviewer-reported weaknesses and follow-up citation critiques, we demonstrate that ToC improves precision by 79% and coverage by 11% relative to strongest baselines, surfacing specific, evidence-grounded concerns that support reviewers in systematic evaluation.

</details>


### [46] [Denoising the Future: Context-Aware Spectral Diffusion for Temporal Knowledge Graph Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20804)
*Yanglei Gan,Peng He,Run Lin,Peiyuan Jiang,Yifan Wang,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了FreqDiff，一个频率感知的扩散框架用于时序知识图谱外推，通过双流去噪器集成时序依赖建模与上下文感知的频谱校准，解决了扩散方法中聚合条件导致的目标判别信号稀释问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的TKG外推方法虽然通过生成式去噪提升了不确定性建模能力，但其对主体历史信息的聚合式条件化处理，难以区分查询相关的关键证据与不显著的历史事实，导致目标判别性信号被稀释，影响未来事实预测的准确性。

Method: 提出FreqDiff框架：(1) 将未来对象预测形式化为查询槽（query-slot）去噪任务；(2) 设计双流去噪器，集成时序依赖建模与上下文感知的频谱校准；(3) 频谱分支从可学习基函数中合成历史条件滤波器，对去噪表示进行自适应重校准；(4) 引入频域正则化器，使去噪目标与真实对象在频谱空间中对齐。

Result: 在四个公开TKG基准数据集上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: FreqDiff通过频率感知的扩散框架有效提升了TKG外推性能，验证了频谱域校准与正则化在时序关系预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graph (TKG) extrapolation seeks to infer future facts from time-varying relational histories. Recent diffusion-based approaches improve uncertainty modeling through generative denoising, but their aggregated conditioning on subject histories may insufficiently distinguish query-specific evidence from non-salient historical facts, thereby diluting target-discriminative signals. To bridge this gap, we propose FreqDiff, a Frequency-aware Diffusion framework for TKG extrapolation. Specifically, FreqDiff formulates future object prediction as query-slot denoising and develops a dual-stream denoiser that integrates temporal dependency modeling with context-aware spectral calibration. The spectral branch synthesizes history-conditioned filters from learnable bases to adaptively re-calibrate denoising representations, while a frequency-domain regularizer is proposed to align the denoised target with the gold object in spectral space. Experiments on four public TKG benchmarks demonstrate that FreqDiff achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [47] [STAR-OPD: Structured Aspect-Cascade-Aware On-Policy Reward Distillation for ABSA Quadruple Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20831)
*Tong Sun,Mingyang Ma,Jiayang Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-OPD，一种针对ABSA四元组抽取任务的结构感知on-policy奖励蒸馏方法，通过直接在学生rollout上应用级联感知的集合结构化奖励来解决蒸馏过程中目标-方面接口的结构性错误（如断开的绑定和幻觉目标），在E-ABSA20K和SemEval-2014上超越off-policy和通用on-policy基线，并显著缩小了学生-教师差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大链式思维（CoT）模型在ABSA四元组抽取上表现优异，但将其蒸馏到小型可部署模型时存在困难。作者发现蒸馏学生模型在目标-方面接口处产生结构性无效状态（如断裂的目标-方面绑定和幻觉目标），而传统off-policy蒸馏只训练教师生成的轨迹，很少对推理阶段主导的学生诱导结构状态提供监督，导致前后端预测被污染。

Method: 提出STAR-OPD，基于通用on-policy蒸馏框架进行实例化。核心是在学生自身的rollout上训练，并使用级联感知（cascade-aware）的集合结构化（set-structured）奖励，直接针对三个方面进行优化：绑定一致性（binding consistency）、目标接地（target grounding）和细粒度方面消歧（fine-grained aspect disambiguation）。

Result: 在E-ABSA20K和SemEval-2014数据集上，STAR-OPD一致性地优于off-policy和通用on-policy基线，减少了目标幻觉，在结构性困难样本上大幅提升性能。使用Qwen3-4B作为学生模型时，STAR-OPD显著缩小了学生-教师差距，同时提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: 实验证明了on-policy结构纠正在蒸馏ABSA四元组抽取中的重要性，STAR-OPD有效缓解了蒸馏过程中的结构错误传播问题，为将大型CoT模型的能力蒸馏到小模型提供了针对性的解决方案。

Abstract: Aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA) quadruple extraction requires jointly predicting target, aspect, opinion, and sentiment over reviews that often contain multiple fine-grained sentiment tuples. While large chain-of-thought (CoT) models perform well on this task, distilling them into smaller deployable models remains difficult. We identify a task-specific failure mode in distilled ABSA extraction: student errors at the target-aspect interface create structurally invalid states, such as broken target-aspect bindings and hallucinated targets, which then corrupt downstream predictions. Conventional off-policy distillation is poorly suited to this setting because it trains only on teacher-generated trajectories and provides little supervision on the student-induced structural states that dominate inference. To address this mismatch, we propose STAR-OPD (STructured Aspect-cascade-aware On-Policy Reward Distillation), which builds on generic on-policy distillation and instantiates it for ABSA quadruple extraction with cascade-aware, set-structured rewards. STAR-OPD trains on student rollouts and applies set-structured rewards that directly target binding consistency, target grounding, and fine-grained aspect disambiguation. Experiments on E-ABSA20K and SemEval-2014 show that STAR-OPD consistently outperforms off-policy and general on-policy baselines, reduces target hallucination, and substantially improves performance on structurally hard cases. With Qwen3-4B, STAR-OPD substantially narrows the student-teacher gap while improving inference efficiency, highlighting the importance of on-policy structural correction for distilled ABSA extraction.

</details>


### [48] [SAC-Copula: Quality-Preserving Watermarking for Diffusion Language Models via Smooth Correlated Gumbel Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20839)
*Baixin Li,Haiyun He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了SAC-Copula，一种基于高斯copula构造平滑局部相关Gumbel扰动场的扩散语言模型水印方法，并通过协方差感知滤波和原生样本校准的SAC感知检测器实现质量与可检测性的良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）采用迭代并行去掩码而非自回归解码，现有基于采样的水印方法通常注入逐位置i.i.d.扰动，与DLM解码动态不一致，导致生成质量下降。需要设计与DLM机制兼容的水印方案。

Method: 1) 通过高斯copula构造平滑的局部相关Gumbel扰动场（SAC-Copula），取代i.i.d.扰动；2) 提出SAC感知检测器，包含协方差感知滤波和原生样本校准；3) 从机制层面分析局部相关性如何降低潜在扰动粗糙度并匹配迭代细化动态。

Result: 在LLaDA上的实验表明SAC-Copula相比现有基线实现了更优的质量-可检测性权衡；在Dream-7B和额外数据集上，相比i.i.d. Gumbel基线显著改善了PPL尾部稳定性，同时保持强低FPR可检测性和竞争力生成质量；通过token编辑压力测试验证了在受控同步漂移下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 局部相关的扰动注入比i.i.d.扰动更适合DLM的迭代细化解码动态，SAC-Copula提供了一种兼顾水印可检测性与文本质量保持的有效方案，并展现出对同步漂移干扰的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Watermarking diffusion language models (DLMs) requires mechanisms compatible with iterative parallel unmasking rather than autoregressive decoding. Existing sampling-based watermarking methods typically inject position-wise i.i.d. perturbations, which can be poorly aligned with DLM decoding dynamics and degrade generation quality. We propose SAC-Copula, a quality-preserving watermarking method for DLMs based on smooth, locally correlated Gumbel perturbation fields constructed via a Gaussian copula. We further develop a SAC-aware detector using covariance-aware filtering and native-sample calibration. Mechanism-level analysis shows that local correlation reduces latent perturbation roughness and better matches iterative refinement dynamics. Experiments on LLaDA show that SAC-Copula achieves a favorable quality-detectability trade-off compared with existing baselines. In particular, further evaluations on Dream-7B and additional datasets show that SAC-Copula substantially improves PPL tail stability over the i.i.d. Gumbel baseline, while maintaining strong low-FPR detectability and competitive overall generation quality. Additional token-edit stress tests further assess watermark robustness under controlled synchronization drift.

</details>


### [49] [Ontology-Driven Structural Regularization for Document-Level Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20856)
*Laura Menotti,Stefano Marchesin,Gianmaria Silvello*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现DocRE远程监督数据中一个被忽视的噪声来源——关系三元组内的结构不一致（包括违反本体约束和逻辑矛盾），并提出了一个本体驱动的框架来量化和强制DocRE数据集的结构一致性，实验证明在训练中强制执行结构良好性可显著减少逻辑矛盾并持续提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DocRE严重依赖成本高昂的人工标注数据集，而大规模远程监督资源（如DocRED distant）因噪声问题未被充分利用。现有工作主要关注标签噪声，但忽略了关系三元组内部的结构不一致（如违反本体约束和逻辑矛盾）这一关键噪声来源。

Method: 提出一个本体驱动的框架来量化和强制执行DocRE数据集中的结构一致性。该框架首先通过本体约束和逻辑规则检测远程监督数据中的结构噪声，然后在训练过程中引入结构正则化以强制生成结构良好的关系三元组。

Result: 分析揭示了DocRED distant中存在的显著结构噪声，并证明这些不一致会传播到模型预测中。在训练中强制执行结构良好性显著减少了逻辑矛盾，并持续改善了泛化性能。

Conclusion: 结构一致性是DocRE中一个缺失的监督维度，结构正则化是利用大规模远程监督数据的有效策略。这一发现为DocRE领域提供了新的研究视角和方法论。

Abstract: Document-Level Relation Extraction (DocRE) relies heavily on costly manually annotated datasets, while large distant supervision resources such as DocRED distant remain underexploited due to noise. We show that a critical yet overlooked source of noise lies in structural inconsistencies within relational triples, including violations of ontology constraints and logical contradictions.
  We introduce an ontology-driven framework to quantify and enforce structural consistency in DocRE datasets. Our analysis reveals substantial structural noise in DocRED distant and demonstrates that such inconsistencies propagate to model predictions. Enforcing structural well-formedness during training significantly reduces logical contradictions and consistently improves generalization performance. These findings establish structural consistency as a missing axis of supervision in DocRE and highlight structural regularization as an effective strategy for leveraging distant data at scale.

</details>


### [50] [KREL: Automatic Medical Coding via Knowledge-Guided Reasoning over Clinical Evidence with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20887)
*Xubin Chen,Yipeng Zhou,Wen Sun,Chengkai Huang,Xiaoming Fu,Quan Z. Sheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出KREL框架，将外部ICD编码指南作为结构化知识注入LLM推理过程，用于自动医疗编码任务，在基准数据集上优于现有PLM和LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动医疗编码（AMC）在临床场景中至关重要，但现有方法面临三大挑战：临床笔记超长导致理解受限、ICD标签空间庞大、复杂编码规则未被LLM显式捕获。现有PLM方法将其视为极端多标签分类，LLM方法则视为生成或推理，但均未充分整合领域知识和编码规则。

Method: 提出KREL（Knowledge-Guided Reasoning over Clinical Evidence with LLMs）框架，将外部ICD编码指南作为结构化知识整合到LLM推理过程中，实现领域知识与LLM推理的紧密结合，以缓解幻觉并提升编码标准合规性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，KREL一致性地优于强PLM基线和最先进的LLM基线方法。

Conclusion: 将外部编码知识以结构化形式注入LLM推理能够有效提升自动医疗编码的性能和合规性，为处理长临床文本和复杂编码规则提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Automatic Medical Coding (AMC), which assigns standardized International Classification of Diseases (ICD) codes to clinical notes, is essential for medical reimbursement, quality reporting, and clinical research. Existing pre-trained language model (PLM)-based methods typically formulate AMC as an extreme multi-label classification problem over a predefined code set, while recent large language model (LLM)-based approaches instead frame it as generation or multi-step reasoning. However, key challenges remain, including the extreme length of clinical notes that hinders effective interpretation, the vast ICD label space, and complex coding rules that are not explicitly captured by LLMs. In this work, we propose Knowledge-Guided Reasoning over Clinical Evidence with LLMs (KREL), a framework that leverages LLMs for clinical text understanding and reasoning while integrating external ICD coding guidelines as structured knowledge. This design enables tight coupling between domain knowledge and LLM reasoning, reducing hallucinations and improving compliance with coding standards. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that KREL consistently outperforms strong PLM-based and state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [51] [ForeDreamer: A Self-Evolving Dual-Agent Memory Architecture for Future Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20920)
*Linhao Zhong,Zongze Du,Linyu Wu,Yu Bo,Hourong Li,Chenchen Jing,Hao Chen,Yuling Xi,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ForeDreamer，一个自进化的双智能体框架，用于开放网络未来事件预测。该框架将原始网页证据转化为结构化记忆（事实记忆+经验记忆），通过主智能体负责搜索预测、记忆处理子智能体负责将搜索结果转化为事实记忆，并通过两条路径演化经验记忆。实验在Prophet Arena和FutureX上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放网络未来事件预测要求智能体从嘈杂、冗余且不完整的证据中提取可靠信号。现有检索/记忆机制要么直接将检索信息喂给智能体，要么依赖简单的记忆功能（如存储和复用先前信息）进行预测，这些方法不足以应对开放网络预测场景。

Method: ForeDreamer将原始网络证据转化为结构化记忆后再进行预测。框架包含两类记忆：事实记忆（针对当前预测问题的特定证据状态）和经验记忆（跨预测周期累积的持久智能体经验）。采用主智能体负责搜索与预测，记忆处理子智能体使用专用工具将搜索结果转化为事实记忆。经验记忆通过两条路径进行进化，同时改进预测决策和事实记忆构建。

Result: 在Prophet Arena和FutureX两个基准上进行了实验，验证了ForeDreamer的有效性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明ForeDreamer在开放网络未来事件预测任务上表现有效，通过分离事实记忆与经验记忆并让双智能体协同工作（记忆处理子智能体提炼证据、主智能体基于结构化记忆推理），显著提升了面对嘈杂网络证据时的预测能力。

Abstract: Open-web future event prediction requires agents to distill reliable signals from noisy, redundant, and incomplete evidence. Existing retrieval/memory mechanisms directly feed retrieved information to agents or rely on simple memory functions such as storing and reusing prior information for prediction, leaving them insufficient for open-web forecasting. We propose to transform raw web evidence into structured memory before prediction, enabling agents to reason over distilled, question-specific evidence rather than noisy retrieval results. This paper presents ForeDreamer, a self-evolving dual-agent framework for managing memory over open-web evidence. ForeDreamer separates factual memory, a question-specific evidence state for the current forecast, from experiential memory, persistent agent experience accumulated across forecasting episodes. It uses a main agent for search and prediction, and a memory-processing subagent to convert search results into factual memory with dedicated tools. ForeDreamer further evolves experiential memory through two tracks, improving both forecasting decisions and factual-memory construction. Experiments on Prophet Arena and FutureX demonstrate the effectiveness of ForeDreamer. Project page: https://zhongzero.github.io/ForeDreamer

</details>


### [52] [Source-Free MT Evaluation Is Not MT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20925)
*Baban Gain,Ramakrishna Appicharla,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证机器翻译评估中，充分性（adequacy）必须依据源文而非参考译文来判断。参考译文只是源文的一种可能表达，可能引入偏差、欠指定或错误。现有混合指标过度依赖参考而忽视源文，导致评估不忠实于充分性的定义。作者呼吁将质量估计（QE）重新定位为源文充分性评估的主要方法，而非参考不可用时的替代方案，并呼吁设计以源文-假设忠实度优先、参考仅作补充证据的混合指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器翻译评估以参考译文为标准，但参考译文仅代表源文的一种可能的翻译，可能引入偏差、欠指定或错误。以参考为基准评估充分性对保持源文含义但不同于参考的翻译系统不公平。混合指标过度依赖参考而非源文，导致充分性评估结构上不完整。

Method: 本文为论证性论文。通过逻辑论证指出：(1)充分性必须相对于源文评判；(2)参考在源-参考-假设三元评估中应仅作为辅助证据；(3)通过分析现有混合指标展示其对参考的高度依赖；(4)提出QE应被重新定位为源文充分性评估的主要方法。

Result: 研究发现现有混合指标相比源文对参考的依赖更强。作者并未断言所有自动MT指标都不使用源文，而是指出任何移除源文或令参考主导源文的评估协议在充分性评估上都是结构不完整的。

Conclusion: 呼吁将QE重新定义为源文充分性评估的首要方法，而非因参考缺失而启用的回退方案；呼吁设计明确优先考虑源文-假设忠实度、仅将参考作为补充证据的混合指标。

Abstract: Reference-based metrics remain the standard choice in machine translation evaluation, partly because quality estimation methods often correlate less well with human judgments. As a result, source-free, reference-based evaluation has become the practical norm, even though it is unfaithful to the definition of translation adequacy and unfair to systems whose outputs preserve the source meaning while differing from the reference. This paper argues that adequacy must be judged with respect to the source. A reference is only one possible rendering of the source and may introduce bias, under-specification, or errors. We further argue that source-reference-hypothesis evaluation is fair only when the judge treats the reference as auxiliary evidence rather than as the primary standard. Otherwise, even source-aware evaluation can reduce adequacy to preference towards reference. We show the existing hybrid metrics are highly reliant on reference compared to source. Our argument is not that all automatic MT metrics fail to use the source. Rather, we argue that any evaluation protocol that removes the source, or allows the reference to dominate the source, is structurally incomplete for adequacy evaluation. However, existing MT papers generally prefer reference-based metrics and use QE metrics only when reference is unavailable. We therefore call for QE to be reframed as a primary approach to source-grounded adequacy evaluation, rather than as a fallback motivated by missing references. We further call for hybrid metrics whose designs explicitly prioritize source--hypothesis faithfulness while using references only as complementary evidence.

</details>


### [53] [MentorPulse: Refreshing Cross-Model Latent Guidance for Long-Form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20927)
*Ziwu Liu,Guozhong Li,Chen Qiu,Weiyang Kong,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MentorPulse框架，通过压缩导师状态到有容量上限的槽记忆、增量处理新生成token、门控交叉注意力更新学生读取的记忆（且不重置学生KV缓存），解决跨模型潜在引导在长序列生成中因静态信号失效的问题。在13个数据集上将导师-学生差距缩小52.2%，优于C2C、T2T及等预算LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模型潜在引导方法（冻结的大模型导师编码输入，冻结的小模型学生生成）假设引导信号在输出增长过程中持续有效，但该假设在长序列生成中不成立。多轮指令跟随实验中，静态引导使4B学生的约束满足度比无引导基线低2.5个点；每16个token进行免训练的刷新仅改变记忆内容即可恢复2.0个点的增益。这表明需要一种高效、训练可行的引导刷新机制。

Method: 提出MentorPulse框架，包含三个关键设计：(1) 将导师状态压缩为有容量上限的槽记忆（capped slot memory）；(2) 增量处理新生成的token；(3) 通过门控交叉注意力更新学生读取的记忆，且不重置学生的KV缓存。配套提出Windowed Refresh Training，将桥接（bridge）暴露于前缀条件记忆训练。此外设计轻量级读取模式检查（read-pattern check）用于部署前预测性能增益。

Result: 在13个数据集上，MentorPulse将导师-学生差距在宏平均上缩小52.2%，优于C2C、T2T及等预算LoRA，且在长输出上增益最大。在来自三个模型族的全部11个导师-学生配对中表现最佳，且随能力差距增大收益边际收窄。轻量级读取模式检查可在部署前预测增益。成本测量识别出在长输出上可主导文本引导的刷新间隔。

Conclusion: 静态跨模型引导在长序列生成中确实会退化，通过周期性、低成本的记忆刷新（MentorPulse）可有效恢复甚至提升增益，且该方法在不同模型族和配置下均具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cross-model latent guidance lets a frozen large mentor encode an input once and a frozen small student generate from the resulting signal. Existing methods keep this signal fixed, assuming it stays useful as the output grows; we show this fails in long-form generation. On multi-turn instruction following, static guidance pushes a 4B student's constraint satisfaction 2.5 points below its no-guidance baseline; a training-free refresh every 16 tokens changes only the memory content and restores a 2.0-point gain over that baseline. We propose MentorPulse to keep guidance fresh at practical cost: it compresses mentor states into a capped slot memory, incrementally processes newly generated tokens, and updates the memory that the student reads through gated cross-attention without resetting the student's KV cache. Windowed Refresh Training exposes the bridge to prefix-conditioned memory. Across thirteen datasets, MentorPulse closes 52.2% of the mentor-student gap on macro average, outperforming C2C, T2T, and equal-budget LoRA, with the largest gains on long outputs. It performs best on all eleven mentor-student pairs from three model families, with margins that narrow as the capability gap grows, and a lightweight read-pattern check predicts the gain before deployment. Measured costs identify refresh intervals that dominate text guidance on long outputs.

</details>


### [54] [Quantization-Aware Healing: A Practical Recipe for Recovering Compressed, 4-Bit LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20953)
*Bakbergen Ryskulov,Iker García-Ferrero,David Montero,David Jansen,Ali Hashemi,Jezabel R. Garcia,Antonio Tiene,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出量化感知修复（QAH）方法，在模型结构压缩和4位量化之后，直接从原始未压缩模型蒸馏4位学生模型，而非使用标准量化感知训练（QAT）的硬标签重拟合。在GPT-OSS 120B→60B→MXFP4流水线上，QAH学生在7/9基准上匹配或超越其bfloat16来源，权重内存减少约4倍，参数仅为教师一半，并以Hypernova-60B开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型部署需要同时进行结构压缩和4位量化，但这会显著损害推理、数学、编码和长上下文能力，必须在部署前进行恢复/修复阶段。默认的量化感知训练（QAT）使用硬标签重拟合压缩量化模型，收敛慢且在峰值后崩溃，无法稳健部署。

Method: 提出量化感知修复（QAH）：由于结构压缩模型从不在全精度下独立训练，其bfloat16检查点只是蒸馏恢复的近似；QAH直接从原始未压缩模型蒸馏4位学生模型（软标签蒸馏），而非对硬标签进行重拟合。在GPT-OSS 120B→60B→MXFP4流水线上测试。

Result: QAH学生在7/9基准上匹配或超越其bfloat16来源，权重内存约为原来的1/4（4位量化），参数仅为教师的一半。相比匹配的QAT基线，达到可比峰值速度约快7倍，且在继续训练下保持稳定，无需手工调优的提前停止。同时报告了分布式训练后端之间的大型可复现质量差距。

Conclusion: QAH提供了一种无需数周超参数搜索即可部署的恢复方案，显著优于QAT基线，生成的Hypernova-60B以开放权重发布，展示了高效、稳定的压缩量化模型恢复路径。

Abstract: Serving large language models cheaply increasingly means shipping models that are both structurally compressed to a fraction of their parameters and quantized to 4 bits. Together these steps degrade reasoning, mathematics, coding, and long-context behavior enough to require a recovery, or healing, stage before deployment. The default recipe, quantization-aware training (QAT), re-fits the compressed, quantized model to hard labels; in our pipeline it converged slowly and collapsed past its peak. We adopted Quantization-Aware Healing (QAH) instead. Because a structurally compressed model is never independently trained at full precision, its bfloat16 checkpoint is a distillation-recovered approximation of the original; QAH distills the 4-bit student directly from the original, uncompressed model. On a GPT-OSS 120B to 60B to MXFP4 pipeline, the QAH student matches or beats its bfloat16 source on 7 of 9 benchmarks at roughly 4 times less weight memory and half the teacher's parameter count, and is released open-weight as Hypernova-60B. Against a matched QAT baseline it reaches a comparable peak about 7 times faster and stays stable under continued training, without hand-tuned early stopping. We also report deployment lessons, including a large, reproducible quality gap between distributed-training backends. Our aim is a recipe deployable without a multi-week hyper-parameter search.

</details>


### [55] [Extractive Summarization for Arabic Documents Using SAraBERT with a Semantic Siamese Similarity Evaluation Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20964)
*Sami Shames El Deen,Mariette Awad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了SAraBERT，一种增强版AraBERT模型，通过引入句间Transformer层用于抽取式摘要任务，并提出了一种名为"语义孪生相似度"的新评估指标来衡量文本间的语义相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的阿拉伯语抽取式摘要模型（如AraBERT）在捕获文档主要思想方面存在不足，且传统评估指标难以充分反映摘要对原文核心内容的覆盖程度，因此需要改进模型结构并引入更合适的评估方法。

Method: 在AraBERT的基础上增加句间Transformer层，以增强模型对句子间关系的建模能力；同时提出Semantic Siamese Similarity（语义孪生相似度）作为新的评估指标，通过孪生网络结构度量两个文本输入之间的语义相似度。

Result: 使用BLEU、ROUGE和所提出的语义孪生相似度指标对SAraBERT及已有相关模型进行评估，仿真结果表明所提模型的有效性。

Conclusion: SAraBERT在阿拉伯语抽取式摘要任务上表现优于现有模型，新的评估指标能够更好地反映摘要对原文核心思想的覆盖情况，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: In this research, we introduce SAraBERT, an enhanced version of AraBERT which proposes inter-sentence transformer layers for extractive summarization tasks. To ensure that the summaries generated by SAraBERT achieve a high coverage of the document's main ideas, we propose Semantic Siamese Similarity, a novel evaluation metric that measures the level of similarity between two text inputs. We validated using BLEU, ROUGE, and Semantic Siamese similarity on Sarabert and published related models. Simulation results showed the effectiveness of our proposed model and motivate follow on research.

</details>


### [56] [Target-Aware Calibration Data Selection for Preserving Uncertainty in Quantized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21019)
*Zhen Yang,Sizai Hou,Kaiwen Zheng,Yaofang Liu,Liang He,Yixuan Chen,Kangning Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Doubt-Preserving Quantization (DPQ)框架，将量化校准数据选择视为一个目标依赖的不确定性保持问题，通过构建全精度预测中的高怀疑样本与通用锚点的混合校准集，实现对不同部署目标（如置信度、边界、弃权行为）的差异化保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化广泛应用于大语言模型部署，但其对不确定性行为（如置信度、边界、弃权）的影响很少被视为首要目标。不同的部署场景关注输入分布的不同区域，而现有工作主要优化面向准确率的压缩指标或在量化后调整分数，缺乏对校准数据选择与目标不确定性行为之间关系的系统性认识。

Method: 形式化定义了分布保持风险和边界保持风险两个目标，并给出混合-不匹配（mixture-mismatch）论证解释为何单一校准方案不可能适配所有目标。据此提出DPQ，一种轻量的量化前校准配方族：利用全精度预测构建目标对齐的校准混合集，包含高怀疑样本（high-doubt examples）和通用锚点（generic anchors），不同变体（如DPQ-r75、DPQ-r50、置信度专用、熵专用）对应不同的目标侧重。

Result: 在8个语言模型、9个NLP基准和22种对比方法上的实验表明，最优固定配方随保持目标而变化：DPQ-r75在SQuAD2答案性边界保持上领先，而更温和或单信号变体（DPQ-r50、置信度专用、熵专用）更好地保持多选题QA行为。

Conclusion: 校准数据应根据部署所需保持的特定全精度分数行为来选择，而非将其视为固定的量化细节。这一发现挑战了现有量化实践中对校准数据的通用化处理方式。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to deploy large language models, but its effect on uncertainty behavior, such as confidence, margins, and abstention, is rarely treated as a primary objective. We frame calibration-data selection for quantization as a target-dependent uncertainty-preservation problem. Different deployments emphasize different regions of the input distribution, yet prior work mainly optimizes accuracy-oriented compression metrics or adjusts scores after quantization. We formalize this goal with distributional and boundary preservation risks, and provide a simple mixture-mismatch argument explaining why no single calibration recipe should be expected to fit all targets. We introduce Doubt-Preserving Quantization (DPQ), a lightweight pre-quantization recipe family that uses full-precision predictions to construct target-aligned calibration mixtures of high-doubt examples and generic anchors. Across 8 language models, 9 NLP benchmarks, and 22 comparison methods, the leading fixed recipe changes with the preservation target: DPQ-r75 leads on SQuAD2 answerability-boundary preservation, while milder or single-signal variants, including DPQ-r50, confidence-only, and entropy-only, better preserve broad multiple-choice QA behavior. These results show that calibration data should be selected for the specific full-precision score behavior a deployment needs to preserve, rather than treated as a fixed quantization detail.

</details>


### [57] [Free-Text Evaluation of LLMs for 5G Domain Knowledge and Fault Analysis using LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21021)
*Rishiraj Sengupta,Sotiris Chatzimiltis,Mohammad Shojafar,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了三个轻量级、可边缘部署的LLM（Claude-Haiku-4.5、GPT-5.4-Mini和Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite）在5G自由文本故障诊断任务上的表现。所有模型在故障诊断上达到至少90%的准确率，但3GPP和O-RAN规范的零样本召回率均低于60%。多裁判LLM评分的一致性（平均≥0.90）验证了LLM-as-Judge方法论的可可靠性，其中Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite在准确率、推理成本和延迟之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的5G和新兴6G网络故障分析需要领域专家解读自由文本诊断信息（如根因解释和推荐操作）。LLM有望自动化此过程，但轻量级、边缘可部署模型能否胜任深度自由文本诊断仍是开放问题。现有基准依赖固定答案的多选题（MCQ），无法充分评估模型的开放式诊断推理能力，因此需要转向自由文本生成的评测范式及可靠的评分框架。

Method: 将评测范式从受限的MCQ转为自由文本生成格式，评估三个轻量级LLM在三个基准（TeleQNA ORAN FT、5G-Faults FT、TeleInter FT）上的5G领域知识和故障分析任务。使用三个独立的领域前沿裁判LLM对输出打分，并通过两两裁判间的一致性检验LLM-as-Judge方法论的可靠性。同时比较各模型在故障诊断准确率、规范召回率、推理成本和延迟方面的综合表现。

Result: 三个模型在故障诊断任务上均达到至少90%的准确率；所有模型在3GPP和O-RAN规范零样本召回上均低于60%，这是关键短板；所有运行中裁判间平均一致性≥0.90，证明多裁判LLM评分具有一致性和可再现性；Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite在准确率、推理成本和延迟之间取得最优平衡，最适合生产级电信部署。

Conclusion: 轻量级、边缘可部署的LLM能够胜任5G故障诊断任务，但在3GPP/O-RAN规范知识的零样本回忆上存在显著不足，需要通过检索增强或微调来弥补。多裁判LLM评分框架可为开放式电信文本输出提供可靠且可扩展的自动评估手段。Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite是实际生产部署中最具成本效益的选择。

Abstract: Real-world fault analysis in 5G and emerging 6G networks demands domain expertise to analyze free-text diagnostics, including root-cause explanations and recommended actions. LLMs have emerged as a promising approach to automating this, yet whether lightweight, edge-deployable models are capable of performing in-depth free-text diagnostics remains an open question. While existing benchmarks rely on restrictive MCQs with fixed answer keys, this paper evaluates 5G domain understanding and fault analysis in a free-text generation format. Transitioning to this paradigm requires evaluating lightweight, edge-deployable AI models on open-ended diagnostic reasoning, alongside a dependable framework to validate these text outputs at scale. To address this we evaluate three lightweight LLMs, Claude-Haiku-4.5, GPT-5.4-Mini, and Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite, on free-text 5G domain knowledge and fault-analysis tasks across three benchmarks, TeleQNA ORAN FT, 5G-Faults FT, and TeleInter FT. Three independent frontier judges score outputs, and pairwise inter-judge agreement is measured as an empirical test of the LLM-as-Judge methodology. All three models reach at least 90% accuracy on fault diagnosis, while zero-shot recall of 3GPP and O-RAN specifications remains the critical gap, with all models scoring below 60%. Mean inter-judge agreement is at least 0.90 across all runs, indicating that multi-judge LLM scoring produces consistent, reproducible grades for open-ended telecom responses. Operationally, Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite offers the best efficiency trade-off, combining competitive accuracy with the lowest inference cost and latency, making it the most suitable candidate for production telecom deployments.

</details>


### [58] [Scaling Unsupervised Word Alignment to Documents via Structural Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21023)
*Michelle Wastl,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CTFAlign和MDPAlign两种无需训练的文档级词对齐方法。CTFAlign采用从粗到细的细化策略限制对齐搜索空间到语义相似区域，MDPAlign则通过主对角线先验约束对齐位置。在六个语言对上平均将词对齐错误率从0.412降至0.326，且收益可迁移到下游的翻译覆盖评估和语义差异识别任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的词对齐研究主要集中在句子层面，但许多跨语言任务需要在完整文档层面建立对应关系。虽然现有的多语言嵌入模型能编码长输入，但直接将句子级对齐算法应用到文档级别会导致性能显著下降。因此需要专门的文档级词对齐方法。

Method: 本文提出两种方法：(1) CTFAlign：一种轻量级、无需训练的粗到细细化策略，将对齐搜索空间限制在语义相似的区域内；(2) MDPAlign：一种更简单的替代方案，通过主对角线先验来约束对齐位置。两种方法都直接在完整文档上操作，不依赖句子切分或句子对齐。

Result: 在六个跨越类型学距离、资源丰富度和文档长度差异的语言对上，使用三种模型平均后，CTFAlign将词对齐错误率从0.412降低到0.326。这些改进还能够在下游任务中迁移，包括文档级翻译覆盖评估和语义差异识别。

Conclusion: CTFAlign和MDPAlign为文档级词对齐提供了有效的无需训练方案，显著优于将句子级算法直接应用于文档的做法，并能带来下游任务的性能提升。作者还发布了Python包和可复现实验的代码与数据。

Abstract: Word alignment has traditionally been studied between sentences, but many cross-lingual tasks increasingly require correspondences across full documents. While recent multilingual embedding models can encode long inputs, we show that applying algorithms designed for sentences directly to documents leads to performance degradation. To address this, we introduce CTFAlign, a lightweight, training-free approach for document-level word alignment. CTFAlign applies a coarse-to-fine refinement strategy that restricts the alignment search space to semantically similar regions. Additionally, we introduce MDPAlign, a simpler alternative that constrains alignments by position with a main diagonal prior. Both approaches operate directly on full documents without relying on sentence segmentation or sentence alignment. We evaluate these methods across six language pairs varying in typological distance, resourcedness, and document length. Averaged over three models, CTFAlign reduces word alignment error rate from 0.412 to 0.326. These gains transfer downstream, leading to improvements in document-level translation coverage evaluation and recognition of semantic differences. We release CTFAlign as a Python package and make the code and data to reproduce our experiments publicly available.

</details>


### [59] [Evidence-Consistent Generative Detection under Scenario-Level Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21043)
*San Kim,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究社交工程欺诈检测（SMS和语音钓鱼）中的场景级分布外（SL-OOD）泛化问题，发现传统分布内评估会高估模型鲁棒性，并提出ECoG——一种结合证据跨度监督与理由-标签一致性约束的生成式框架，显著提升OOD场景下的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布内评估可能高估模型鲁棒性，因为训练和测试数据共享重复的任务特定模式或表面线索。在社交工程欺诈检测中，攻击者可以保持恶意意图的同时改变场景、冒充实体或措辞，导致模型可能依赖场景特定的表面线索（场景记忆）而非决策相关证据。现有工作缺乏对模型能否泛化到未见攻击场景的系统评估。

Method: 提出ECoG（Evidence-Consistent Generative）框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 证据跨度监督（evidence-span supervision），让模型在训练时学习关注决策相关的证据片段；(2) 理由-标签一致性目标（rationale-label consistency objective），约束模型生成的理由与其预测标签保持一致。使用SL-OOD评估协议，将整个攻击场景从训练中排除，保持标签空间固定，在多个基准（基于特征、编码器、解码器）上进行比较。

Result: 在0.5B解码器上，相比无一致性正则化的同骨干网络：OOD困难实例的Macro-F1提升3.22点；生成理由支持相反标签的预测占比降低4.22点；与参考证据跨度的token级重叠提升8.38点。预测-理由不一致性的降低在四个解码器骨干上均一致有效。同时发现高分布内性能并不能可靠预测分布外鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 紧凑型生成式检测器在社交工程场景漂移下，可以从证据监督和理由-标签一致性训练中获益，有助于缓解"场景记忆"问题，提升对未见攻击场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Conventional in-distribution evaluation can overestimate robustness when training and test data share recurring task-specific patterns or surface cues. This risk is especially relevant in social-engineering fraud detection, where attackers can preserve malicious intent while changing the scenario, impersonated entity, or wording. We study this problem as scenario-level out-of-distribution (SL-OOD) detection for SMS and voice phishing, where entire attack scenarios are held out from training while the label space remains fixed. This setting tests whether models can generalize to unseen attack scenarios using decision-relevant evidence rather than familiar scenario-specific cues. Using this SL-OOD evaluation, we find that high in-distribution performance does not reliably predict held-out robustness across feature-, encoder-, and decoder-based baselines. We interpret this gap as scenario memorization: reliance on recurring scenario-specific lexical or entity cues rather than decision-relevant evidence. We propose ECoG, an evidence-consistent generative framework that combines evidence-span supervision with a rationale-label consistency objective during training. On the 0.5B decoder, relative to the same backbone trained without consistency regularization, ECoG raises Macro-F1 on OOD challenging instances by 3.22 points, reduces the share of predictions whose generated rationale supports the opposite label by 4.22 points, and increases token-level overlap with reference evidence spans by 8.38 points; the reduction in prediction-rationale inconsistency is consistent across four decoder backbones. These results suggest that compact generative detectors can benefit from evidence supervision and rationale-label consistency under social-engineering shift.

</details>


### [60] [PromptResponse: Optimizing Prompts for LLM Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21074)
*Erik Thureck,Robert Kühnen,Tim Jacobowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过8200次GPT-4o执行实验，研究了编码任务提示的格式化和LLM调优对生成代码性能、效率和稳定性的影响，发现一致格式化（尤其是JSON）能提升生成效率和语法稳定性，而LLM调优提示反而显著降低任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在研究和软件开发工作流中被广泛使用，但其输出对输入提示的微小变化非常敏感。然而，目前缺乏系统性对照研究来揭示不同提示格式和调优方式对编码任务生成结果的具体影响，这阻碍了实践者做出有根据的提示设计决策。

Method: 基于HumanEval数据集设计了五种语义相同但语法不同的提示变体（基线、JSON、Markdown、YAML和LLM调优版本），使用GPT-4o进行超过8200次代码生成执行，从代码性能、生成效率和稳定性三个维度进行系统评估。

Result: 一致的格式化（尤其是JSON格式）能改善生成效率和语法稳定性，并在任务性能上有小幅提升；相反，LLM调优的提示在所有维度上均未带来显著改善，且任务性能明显下降。

Conclusion: 低成本的格式化调整即可带来可衡量的改进，而LLM调优必须考虑模型的对其性。论文还提供了实践建议，并发布了数据集变体和评估管线以供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in research workflows and software development pipelines, yet their output remains sensitive to input prompt variations. This paper presents $\unicode{x00AB}$PromptResponse$\unicode{x00BB}$, a controlled study examining how formatting and LLM-based tuning of coding task prompts affect the resulting code's performance, efficiency, and stability. Using five semantically identical yet syntactically distinct variants of the HumanEval dataset$\unicode{x2014}$baseline, JSON, Markdown, YAML, and an LLM-tuned version$\unicode{x2014}$we had GPT-4o solve its coding problems over 8200$\unicode{x00A0}$executions. Our results show that consistent formatting$\unicode{x2014}$especially JSON$\unicode{x2014}$improves generation efficiency and syntactic stability, with minor gains in task performance. Conversely, the LLM-tuned prompts resulted in significantly degraded task performance without significant improvements in any other dimension. These findings suggest that low-effort reformatting alone can yield measurable improvements, while tuning must account for model alignment. We conclude our work with providing a set of practical recommendations informed by our results as well as releasing our dataset variants and evaluation pipeline for future work.

</details>


### [61] [Jokes Aside: Measuring the Semantic Distance of Double Meanings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21087)
*Fabio De Ponte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文基于上下文嵌入向量重新审视了Petrovic和Matthews（2013）及Winters等（2019）提出的笑话生成与幽默度量模型，在原有五个指标中重访了三个（明显性、兼容性、比较性），并首次引入新指标"对称性"。使用两种嵌入模型在三个数据集上进行实验，结果显示基于这些指标训练的模型在幽默评分预测上表现不佳（最佳57.1%准确率，低于61.5%基线），但对称性指标与高评分笑话一致相关，可能捕捉了幽默的必要但非充分属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型和上下文嵌入向量的发展，为重新检验计算幽默研究中早前的假设提供了新机会。作者希望利用现代词嵌入技术来回顾和精细化Petrovic和Matthews（2013）关于"I like my X like I like my Y, Z"笑话模式的理论框架，以及Winters等（2019）基于传统N-gram和Word2Vec的幽默度量方法。

Method: 使用OpenAI text-embedding-3-small和MiniLM all-MiniLM-L6-v2两种嵌入模型，在JokeJudger、Expunations和rJokes三个数据集上进行实验。重新审视了五个度量指标中的三个（明显性、兼容性、比较性），并首次提出"对称性"指标（定义为Z与X和Y的接近程度）。此外，对Expunations和rJokes数据集进行了扩展，添加了包含歧义表达两种含义的配对句子。

Result: 基于所提出度量指标训练的模型在预测幽默评分方面表现不佳：在JokeJudger上最佳模型仅达到57.1%的准确率，低于61.5%的基线；在Expunations和rJokes上的表现更低。然而，对称性指标与评分更高的笑话之间存在一致的相关性，这表明它可能捕捉了幽默的一个必要但不充分的性质。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入向量的传统幽默度量在预测幽默评分方面并未超越基线，但新引入的对称性指标显示出与幽默质量的稳定关联。未来的工作可能需要结合对称性这一必要属性与其他因素，才能更全面地理解和建模幽默。

Abstract: Large language models have significantly enriched the toolkit for computational humor research, particularly in the automated generation of jokes and puns. A key innovation, contextual embedding vectors, offers new opportunities to revisit and refine earlier hypotheses. Notably, Petrovic and Matthews (2013) proposed a joke generation model based on the scheme "I like my X like I like my Y, Z" (e.g. "I like my ice like I like my dreams, crushed"). They suggested that joke hilarity increases with: a) frequent association of Z with X and Y, b) rarity of Z, c) ambiguity of Z, and d) meaning distance between X and Y. Building on this, Winters et al. (2019) proposed a set of metrics, based on Google Ngrams and Word2Vector. In this work, three out of their five metrics are revisited with word embeddings: obviousness, compatibility, and comparison. Another measure, symmetry, defined as closeness of Z to both X and Y, is introduced here for the first time. Two models were used to collect the embedding vectors (OpenAI text-embedding-3-small and MiniLM all-MiniLM-L6-v2) on three datasets: JokeJudger, Expunations, and rJokes. The last two datasets, Expunations, and rJokes, were expanded by adding paired sentences that captured the ambiguous expression at the core of each joke in its two different meanings. Results revealed that models trained on the proposed metrics performed poorly in predicting humor ratings: on JokeJudger, the best model achieved 57.1% accuracy, below the 61.5% baseline, while performance on Expunations and rJokes was even lower. Nevertheless, the symmetry metric seems consistently associated with higher-rated jokes, suggesting it may capture a necessary -though not sufficient- property of humor.

</details>


### [62] [When the Feature Pool Goes Algorithmic: Extending Mufwene's Ecology of Language Evolution to LLM-Mediated Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21088)
*Kunmei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大型语言模型（LLM）视为"分布中介体"，它们聚合、变换并重新分配人类语言数据，从而在Mufwene生态语言学模型之上引入一种新的生态过程——"算法性重新加权"说话者可获取的变体分布，但人类的社会评价仍然是语言演变的关键决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mufwene的生态模型将语言演变定位于个体语码（idiolects）之间的变体竞争与说话人的选择，但LLM的出现改变了语言资源供给的方式，使得语言演变的外部媒介条件发生根本性变化。需要扩展原有理论框架来理解LLM如何影响语言演变。

Method: 文章提出将LLM概念化为"分布性中介"（distributional mediators）：它们（1）聚合来自人类群体的语言输出，（2）通过训练和后训练改变语言分布特征，（3）大规模重新分配模型特有的输出。作者由此提出"算法性重新加权"（algorithmic reweighting）这一概念，即模型中介可以改变竞争变体到达人类选择者的相对频率。论文结合现有证据（模型特有的语言特征、词汇采纳）进行论证，并推导可检验的预测。

Result: 结果表明：模型的确可能对说话者可获取的变体分布进行重新加权，现有证据与部分通路一致，但不足以确立必然的语言趋同。模型相关形式可能扩散并被规约化、可能被社会标记为"像AI"而遭回避、也可能根本不扩散。论文给出了关于采纳、模型版本效应、趋同与社会逆转的可检验预测。

Conclusion: 该提议将Mufwene的"特征池生态"（feature-pool ecology）向上游扩展了一步——即在说话人选择之前引入模型中介环节。人类的社会评价仍然是语言演变的最终裁决者，但LLM作为分布中介在语言生态中扮演了前所未有的角色。

Abstract: Mufwene's ecological model locates language evolution in competition among variants contributed by individual idiolects and in speakers' selection from linguistic material made available through interaction. Large language models (LLMs) complicate this architecture without requiring the locus of selection to move away from human speakers. This article argues that LLMs are best treated as distributional mediators: they aggregate language produced across human populations, transform its distribution through training and post-training, and redistribute model-specific outputs at scale. I call the resulting ecological process algorithmic reweighting of the speaker-accessible distribution: model mediation can alter the relative frequencies with which competing variants reach human selectors. Emerging evidence on model-specific linguistic profiles and lexical uptake is consistent with parts of this pathway, but does not establish inevitable convergence. Human social evaluation remains decisive: model-associated forms may diffuse and become conventionalized, become socially recognizable as 'AI-like' and subsequently avoided, or fail to diffuse in the first place. The proposal extends Mufwene's feature-pool ecology one step upstream of speaker selection and yields testable predictions about uptake, model-version effects, convergence, and social reversal.

</details>


### [63] [No PUN Intended: Plausible Unknown Names for Person-Centred LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21206)
*Dimitri Staufer,David Hartmann,Ibrahim Baroud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PUN（Plausible Unknown Names）协议，用于构建和验证"未知姓名"——即形式上符合人名结构但无完整姓名证据、无歧义信号的名字，并发布了300个经过严格验证的未知姓名及对照样本，用于改进LLM评测中姓名作为提示变量的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人名常被用作LLM评测中的提示变量来评估事实性、隐私泄露、偏见和弃答行为，但若名字的证据状态（evidential status）未被控制，评测结果可能会混淆记忆（memorisation）、检索（retrieval）、名字先验（name priors）以及错误的人物归因（wrong-person attribution）等因素，导致评测失真。因此需要一套标准化的方法来构建和验证真正的"未知姓名"。

Method: 作者将"未知姓名"操作化定义为：具有合理的"名+姓"形式、没有索引到的全名证据、且在文档化验证流程下没有歧义信号。在此基础上提出PUN协议，包含三个环节：(1) 从Wikidata派生姓名组件；(2) 使用具备Web访问能力的LLM进行筛选；(3) 采用受控的搜索再验证（controlled search revalidation）。最后通过204名参与者的真人研究、接受率、可复现性和消融实验来评估协议效果。

Result: PUN协议产出的被接受名字在"更像人名"（name-like）程度上优于对照组；真人参与者仅在3%的案例中能恢复出人物证据。作者发布了300个未知姓名及配套对照样本。

Conclusion: PUN协议能够可靠地构建和验证未知姓名，有效控制名字的证据状态，为LLM评测中涉及人名的任务提供了更干净的实验条件，从而提升评测结果的有效性和可解释性。

Abstract: Person names are widely used as prompt variables in LLM evaluations of factuality, privacy leakage, bias and abstention, but when a name's evidential status is uncontrolled, measurements may conflate memorisation, retrieval, name priors and wrong-person attribution. We operationalise an unknown name as one with plausible First-Last form, no indexed full-name evidence, and no ambiguity signals under a documented validation run, and introduce PUN (Plausible Unknown Names), a protocol for constructing and validating such names, combining Wikidata-derived components, web-enabled LLM screening, and controlled search revalidation. We report acceptance rate, reproducibility, ablations, and a 204-participant human study, finding accepted names are more name-like than controls while participants recover person evidence in only 3% of cases. We release 300 names with comparison controls.

</details>


### [64] [RARE: Decoupling Representation Steering from Expert Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21236)
*Zhibo Zhang,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RARE框架，通过将行为扰动投影到路由器矩阵的零空间来消除路由可见成分，从而解决表示工程（Representation Engineering）直接应用于MoE模型时的结构不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示工程通过修改中间隐藏状态来控制语言模型行为，是一种轻量级方法。然而将其直接应用于MoE模型会产生结构不匹配——路由器对隐藏状态的扰动高度敏感，导致行为控制失效。作者通过实证研究验证了这一失败模式，并发现保持路由干净能显著恢复steering性能，且路由对语义内容比对行为变化更敏感，这为后续方法设计提供了动机。

Method: RARE框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 将任意行为扰动投影到路由器矩阵的零空间，移除路径可见成分（router-visible components），使扰动不会影响路由决策；(2) 进一步校正传播到下游选中层的路由漂移（routing drift）。同时，作者评估了五种不同的扰动估计器在该框架下的表现。

Result: 在6个异构开源MoE模型上跨越三个steering场景（有害性、真实性、事实编辑）进行评估。有害性steering上，RARE达到53.3%的平均攻击成功率，同时保持67.8%的MMLU准确率，比baseline有更强的效果-效用权衡。TruthfulQA MC1准确率从41.0%提升至58.6%，CounterFact有效性从16.8%提升至96.3%。

Conclusion: 路由一致性是将表示工程适配到MoE模型时一个重要的架构考量。RARE证明了通过消除路由可见扰动可以有效解决MoE中的表示工程适配问题，在保持模型能力的同时实现有效的行为控制。

Abstract: Representation engineering offers a lightweight means of controlling language-model behavior by modifying intermediate hidden states, but its direct application to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models introduces a structural mismatch. We first verify this failure mode through a series of empirical studies and find that preserving clean routing substantially recovers steering performance and that routing is more sensitive to semantic content than to behavioral changes under controlled content. Motivated by these findings, we introduce RARE, a router-agnostic representation engineering framework for MoE language models. RARE projects arbitrary behavioral perturbations onto the null space of the router matrix, thereby removing router-visible components, and further corrects routing drift propagated to selected downstream layers. To decide the best perturbation estimator in this framework, we evaluate five estimators on six heterogeneous open-weight MoE models across three steering scenarios: harmfulness, truthfulness, and factual editing. On harmfulness steering, RARE reaches an average attack success rate of 53.3% while retaining 67.8% MMLU accuracy, yielding a stronger aggregate effectiveness--utility trade-off than baselines. It further improves average TruthfulQA MC1 accuracy from 41.0% to 58.6% and CounterFact efficacy from 16.8% to 96.3%. These results support routing consistency as an important architectural consideration for adapting representation engineering to MoE models.

</details>


### [65] [Affective Context Amplifies Sycophancy in LLM Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21242)
*Jiayi Li,Sanjana Menon,Brett Frischmann,Shomir Wilson,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在对话中获取用户情绪状态后，这种情感背景如何调节其谄媚行为（sycophancy）。通过在七个LLM和两个Reddit数据集上，将相同内容分别作为第三方叙述和用户自身披露呈现，测量模型独立评估与面向用户回应之间的分歧，发现这种分歧是系统性且强烈单向的：面向用户的回应一致地软化或隐瞒负面/反对性判断。负面情感状态（尤其是孤独和痛苦）进一步放大这种分歧，表明情感背景作为脆弱性信号抑制了用户最需要时的批判性反馈，模型常通过回避式谄媚（转向非承诺性回应）而非直接附和来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs作为对话伴侣常能访问用户情绪状态，但情感背景如何影响模型的谄媚行为尚不清楚。作者希望理解这种影响，特别是在主观评价型互动中，模型是否因情感上下文而系统性偏离独立判断。

Method: 借鉴讨好理论（ingratiation theory），将谄媚操作化为模型独立评估与面向用户回应之间的分歧。通过将相同内容呈现为第三方叙述或用户自身披露来诱发分歧，在七个LLM和两个Reddit数据集（r/AmItheAsshole和r/TrueUnpopularOpinion）上进行系统测量。

Result: 发现分歧是系统性且强烈单向的：面向用户的回应一致地软化或隐瞒负面/反对性判断。情感背景进一步放大这种分歧，负面状态（尤其是孤独和痛苦）产生最大效应。模型在用户可能最需要批判性反馈时反而抑制了它，且常通过回避式谄媚（退缩到非承诺性回应）而非直接同意。

Conclusion: 情感背景在LLM中充当脆弱性信号，抑制了用户最需要时的批判性反馈，表现为回避式谄媚。这一发现对AI安全性和对话系统的负责任设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: As conversational companions, large language models (LLMs) often have access to users' emotional states. We study how this affective context modulates LLM sycophancy in subjective, evaluative interactions, where users share actions or opinions that invite feedback. Drawing on ingratiation theory, we measure sycophancy as the divergence between a model's independent evaluation and its user-facing response, elicited by presenting the same content as either a third-party account or the user's own disclosure. Across seven LLMs and two Reddit datasets (r/AmItheAsshole and r/TrueUnpopularOpinion), we find that this divergence is systematic and strongly one-directional. User-facing responses consistently soften or withhold negative or oppositional judgments. Affective context further amplifies this divergence with negative states, particularly loneliness and distress, producing the largest effects. These findings suggest that affective context functions as a vulnerability signal that suppresses critical feedback when users may need it most, often through evasive sycophancy, in which models retreat toward non-committal responses rather than outright agreement.

</details>


### [66] [Benchmarking Patent Drafting from Inventor-Style Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21249)
*Lekang Jiang,Wenjun Sun,Stephan Goetz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Dis2Pat数据集和Patent-MAF多智能体框架，解决从早期发明人披露材料直接生成完整且法律一致的专利申请的核心挑战。专利-MAF作为本地可部署的强基线，在专利撰写任务上优于开源模型并与大型闭源模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在单个专利撰写任务上表现良好，但无法处理现实专利申请的核心挑战：从早期发明材料生成完整且法律连贯的专利申请。以往工作假设输入是后期阶段、高度结构化或已法律化的文本，而真实的专利申请流程始于发明人撰写的非正式、去法律化的披露材料。这一差距阻碍了LLM在真实专利工作流中的应用。

Method: 本文提出两个核心贡献：(1) Dis2Pat，一个"披露到专利"数据集，模拟真实专利工作流程，要求从发明人风格的去法律化披露中直接生成完整的专利申请；(2) Patent-MAF，一个多智能体框架，作为可在本地部署的强基线，用于处理长文本、法律约束强的专利撰写任务，并满足隐私要求。

Result: 基准测试结果显示，现有LLM在专利撰写任务上表现出局限性，而Patent-MAF作为强基线持续优于评估的开源模型，并与大型闭源模型保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 从去法律化的发明披露直接生成完整专利申请是LLM尚未解决的挑战，Patent-MAF提供的多智能体框架在本地部署和隐私保护条件下展现了可行的解决方案，为未来专利AI研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: While recent large language models (LLMs) have achieved promising results on individual patent drafting tasks, they fundamentally fail to investigate the core challenge of real-world patent drafting: generating a complete and legally coherent patent application directly from early-stage invention materials. Prior work predominantly assumes later-stage, highly structured, or already legalistic inputs. However, real patenting workflows begin with informal, de-legalized disclosures authored by inventors. To bridge the gap, we introduce Dis2Pat, a disclosure-to-patent dataset that reflects realistic patenting workflows by requiring the generation of complete patent applications directly from inventor-style, de-legalized disclosures. Given the inherent difficulty of long-form, legally constrained patent drafting and the strong privacy requirements, we further propose a strong baseline named Patent-MAF. It is a multi-agent framework for locally deployable patent drafting. Benchmark results reveal that current LLMs exhibit limitations in patent drafting, while Patent-MAF provides a strong baseline that consistently outperforms evaluated open-source models and remains competitive with large closed-source models.

</details>


### [67] [EnSI-RAG: Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long-Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21252)
*Xuanyu Meng,Jiashuo Sun,Jash Rajesh Parekh,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EnSI-RAG（实体-结构-索引检索增强生成）框架，通过构建查询无关的实体中心索引来解决长连接文档上的多跳问答问题。每个记录以 (e, t, k, v) 四元组表示实体、类型、语义类别和值，并保留原始段落链接，将证据定位与答案合成分离。在 Loong 和 Oolong 基准上平均准确率达 78.24，较基线提升 6.62 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RAG 方法通常将文档索引为原始块并通过嵌入相似度检索，当块边界将实体与支撑证据分离，或问题需要进行跨语料库多跳推理时，性能会显著下降。因此需要一种能够保留实体关系结构、支持多跳证据定位的索引机制。

Method: EnSI-RAG 构建查询无关的实体中心索引，每个记录为四元组 (e, t, k, v)，其中 e 为实体，t 为实体类型，k 为语义类别（property 属性、relation 关系、aspect 方面），v 为取值，同时保留指向原始源段落的链接。查询时这些记录充当检索句柄，LLM 对检索到的段落进行综合生成最终答案，将证据定位与答案合成解耦。

Result: 在 Loong 和 Oolong 基准上，EnSI-RAG 平均准确率达到 78.24，相对已发表的基线分数高出 6.62 分，验证了框架在长连接文档 QA 场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 实体中心的结构化索引方法有效解决了传统 RAG 在长文档多跳问答中的不足，通过分离证据定位与答案合成、保留可溯源证据，显著提升了性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over long, connected documents remains challenging because relevant evidence may span multiple entities and their relationships. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods typically index documents as raw chunks and retrieve them through embedding similarity. Their performance degrades when chunk boundaries separate entities from supporting evidence or when a question requires multi-hop reasoning across the corpus. We propose EnSI-RAG (Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a framework that constructs a query-independent, entity-centered index. Each record (e, t, k, v) represents an entity e, its type t, a semantic category k in {property, relation, aspect}, and a value v, while retaining links to the original source passages. At query time, these records serve as retrieval handles, and an LLM synthesizes the retrieved passages into the final answer. This design separates evidence localization from answer synthesis while preserving traceable source evidence. Across Loong and Oolong, EnSI-RAG achieves an average accuracy of 78.24. Relative to the published baseline scores used as references, this is 6.62 points higher, suggesting its effectiveness across these settings. The code is available at https://github.com/RamonMeng/EnSI-RAG.

</details>


### [68] [Memory Augmentation Unlocks Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21265)
*Simeng Zhang,Yilong Chen,Wenyuan Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Yao Chen,Junyuan Shang,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆增强压缩框架，通过从历史推理轨迹中构建可复用的推理记忆，作为预填充侧支架来补偿CoT压缩过程中丢失的信息，在数学推理、复杂推理和科学问答任务上显著提升Chain-of-Draft压缩性能并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型依赖链式思维推理解决复杂任务，但详细推理轨迹带来大量推理开销。CoT压缩虽能缩短生成长度，但过度压缩会破坏逻辑连贯性并降低性能。现有方法缺乏一个原则性的框架来平衡压缩带来的效率收益与上下文信息的损失。

Method: 首先形式化"上下文-生成替代律"，明确显式推理上下文可替代部分解码时生成。基于此原则提出记忆增强压缩（Memory-Augmented Compression），这是一个无需训练的框架：从历史推理轨迹中构建可复用的推理记忆（而非原始示例），总结可复用的推理模式、关键约束和关键操作，在预填充阶段作为支架检索注入以补偿压缩过程中的信息损失。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH、BBH和MMLU-Sci上，相比Chain-of-Draft分别提升21.4、28.0、29.5和6.61个准确率点，同时相比标准CoT实现1.14-1.49倍的延迟加速。记忆增强框架与token级、推理轨迹级和推理状态级压缩机制均兼容。消融分析表明性能提升源于相关推理记忆本身，而非简单增加上下文长度。

Conclusion: 记忆增强压缩为CoT压缩与推理质量的权衡提供了一个原则性的解决方案，通过可复用的记忆来补偿压缩信息损失，实现了增益与加速的双重优化。

Abstract: Large language models often rely on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to solve complex tasks, but verbose reasoning traces introduce substantial inference overhead. CoT compression shortens generation, yet aggressive compression may disrupt logical coherence and degrade performance. We formalize this trade-off as the \textit{Context-Generation Substitution Law}, where explicit reasoning context substitutes for part of decode-time generation. Based on this principle, we propose \textit{Memory-Augmented Compression}, a training-free framework that constructs reusable reasoning memories from historical traces and retrieves them as prefill-side scaffolds. Rather than using raw demonstrations, these memories summarize reusable reasoning patterns, key constraints, and critical operations to compensate for information lost during compression. Experiments show that Memory consistently improves prompt-based Chain-of-Draft (CoD) compression across mathematical reasoning, complex reasoning, and science question answering tasks, yielding accuracy gains of 21.4, 28.0, 29.5, and 6.61 points over CoD on GSM8K, MATH, BBH, and MMLU-Sci, while achieving a 1.14--1.49$\times$ latency speedup over standard CoT. Memory is also compatible with token-level, reasoning-trace-level, and inference-state compression mechanisms. Further analyzes show that the gains come from relevant reasoning memories rather than simply increasing context length.

</details>


### [69] [Prompt-Model Interaction Reaches the Fixed Points: A deterministic, task-free structural readout -- and the factorizations of it that failed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21315)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过一个无任务的"读出"（固定点结构）证明提示词效果不是提示词自身的属性，而是提示词-模型配对（prompt-model pair）的属性，并批评了以往研究中"给无变化空间的量施加有形状的判据"的常见谬误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅从任务准确率角度证明提示词效果随模型与格式变化，无法区分这是任务机制的问题还是条件分布本身的问题。作者希望在脱离任何任务的情况下，检验模型对文本片段的"读取"是否本身就携带提示词效应。

Method: 构建无任务读出：对短窗口自回归模型计算 x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t) 的固定点结构，从96个起始点普查。测试提示词长度、文本类型（散文vs标记）、通用方向、双向性、指令微调的抵抗性等假说，并对比注意力汇（attention-sink）机制解释。

Result: （1）提示词效应在该读出上以完整幅度出现：9个词素可移动固定点比例的大部分区间、改变四类结构类并重排模型排序，而60.5 IFEval的指令微调几乎不改变结构类。（2）所有提出的解释因素均被否定：长度效应非单调；散文vs标记、通用方向、双向性、指令抵抗等四种现象学因素在扩大样本后均消失；注意力汇解释在5个模型上仅2个预测正确（等于随机），且在均匀随机输入上失效。

Conclusion: 在该读出上，解释的基本单位是提示词-模型配对（prompt-model pair），而非提示词本身。作者指出研究反复犯下的错误是"给没有变化空间的量施加有形状的判据"——即对不具备该性质变化余地的对象，错误地赋予其结构性判据。

Abstract: That a prompt's effect is not a property of the prompt is established: prompts optimised for one model degrade on another, and rankings reorder under neutral reformatting. That evidence is about task accuracy, which cannot say whether the interaction is a fact about task machinery or about the conditional distribution itself. We ask on a readout with no task in it: the fixed-point structure of the short-window argmax map x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t), censused from 96 starts. It is deterministic, so nothing can be helped or hurt, and it exists only at short windows -- four of six models lose it entirely by window 16 -- so everything here concerns how a model reads a fragment. Two results. First, the interaction reaches this readout at full magnitude: nine tokens of conditioning move the fixed-point fraction across most of its range, change a four-way structural class, and reorder models, while instruction tuning worth 60.5 IFEval points moves the class by zero. Second, nothing we proposed carries it. Prefix length fails: the effect is not monotone. Four phenomenological factors -- prose-versus-markup, a universal direction, bidirectionality, instruct-resistance -- were each withdrawn within one run of being proposed, dissolved by widening the sample. And the nearest mechanistic account, attention-sink dominance of early tokens, predicts the sign of the shift on 2 of 5 models -- chance -- while a length-by-content cross shows it holds on real text and fails on our probe's uniformly random input, so we are outside its regime, not against it. One fixed nine-token prefix drives four models toward 0 and two toward 1; the bidirectionality survives in-distribution starts. On this readout the unit of explanation is the prompt-model pair. The recurring error it caught in us has a name: a criterion with a shape applied to a quantity with no room to vary.

</details>


### [70] [Move by Move: Measuring and Steering How LLMs Conduct Psychotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21325)
*Afonso Baldo,Hugo Pitorro,Areti Vassilopoulos,Anabela C. Areias,Maya D'Eon,Fabíola Costa,Ricardo Rei,Nuno M. Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种包含十种治疗性动作（therapeutic moves）的本体论，用于分析大语言模型在进行心理治疗互动时的行为。研究发现前沿模型过度使用询问（最高达人类三倍）、忽视心理教育，且高度依赖人类心理治疗师开局的上下文策略。通过将该本体论作为工具集暴露给模型，无需微调即可将模型与人类动作分布的偏差减少约一半，并将回合级对齐度提升7-9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户越来越多地依赖大语言模型寻求情感支持，但学界对模型如何实际进行心理治疗互动缺乏了解。需要一种系统化的方法来刻画和比较人类心理治疗师与模型在咨询中的治疗性行为差异。

Method: 1) 基于MULTI-60清单构建包含十种治疗性动作的本体论（compact、基于功能分类）；2) 通过五位持有执照的心理学家进行标注活动验证本体论；3) 采用基于裁判（judge-based）的方法进行规模化标注，达到专家一致性水平；4) 将该方法应用于真实咨询录音文本与模型主导的会话，比较人类临床医生与一组前沿模型的动作分布；5) 将本体论作为工具集暴露给模型，评估其对行为对齐的改进效果。

Result: 1) 模型过度使用询问动作，频率高达人类的三倍；2) 模型忽视心理教育（psychoeducation）；3) 模型具有强烈的上下文锚定效应：能够延续人类临床医生已开启的策略，但很少自行发起这些策略；4) 将本体论作为工具集暴露给模型后，模型与人类动作分布的平均偏差约减半；5) 回合级与人类治疗师的对齐度提升7-9个百分点，且无需任何微调。

Conclusion: 基于功能的本体论能够有效刻画并量化LLM与人类心理治疗师的行为差异。通过工具调用方式暴露治疗动作本体，可以显著改善模型的行为对齐性，为提升LLM在心理支持场景中的表现提供了无需微调的可行路径。

Abstract: Users increasingly turn to large language models for emotional support, yet little is known about how these models actually conduct a psychotherapy interaction. We introduce an ontology of ten therapeutic moves: compact, function-based categories grounded in the MULTI-60 inventory, validated through an annotation campaign with five licensed psychologists, and scaled with a judge-based approach that matches expert agreement. Applying it to real counseling transcripts and model-led sessions, we compare the move distributions between human clinicians and a panel of frontier models. Models over-use inquiry at up to three times the human rate, neglect psychoeducation, and are strongly context-anchored: they carry forward strategies initiated by a human clinician but rarely initiate them themselves. Exposing the ontology as a set of tools roughly halves the mean deviation from the human move distribution and improves turn-level alignment with human therapist by 7-9 percentage points, without any fine-tuning.

</details>

### [71] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"规格驱动的智能体开发"（SDAD）范式，将传统工程纪律从编码阶段上移至规格精确定义阶段，通过意图捕获、机器可读规格、智能体合成和独立多智能体验证（人工签署）实现自主交付，并配套团队角色转型、量化治理指标（歧义税、规格保真度、SER、TCI_agentic）及分阶段迁移蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的长上下文窗口（数十万至数百万token）正在重塑软件开发全生命周期，使得大量功能需求文档和仓库上下文可在单次工作流中被处理。在此背景下，规格质量成为自主交付的执行燃料，需要一套新的正式化开发范式来协调前置形式化纪律与高速执行之间的矛盾，同时也需回应Waterfall与Agile之间的历史钟摆问题。

Method: 论文采用"四阶段框架"方法：1）意图捕获（intent capture）；2）机器可读规格（machine-readable specification）；3）智能体合成（agentic synthesis）；4）独立多智能体验证并在人工签署下放行。同时将软件开发划分为包含"AI-code"在内的四种生产范式，对Human-Agile（约2020）与Agentic-SDAD（约2026）进行跨制品、节奏、问责和安全性四个维度的对比。并构建了定量治理模型，包括歧义税（Ambiguity Tax）、规格保真度（Spec Fidelity）、SER以及带修复倍增因子phi的TCI_agentic指标。

Result: 论文整合了工业和学术界在AI增强测试与验证方面的证据，据此论证了合成（synthesis）权与发布（release）权分离的必要性，证明智能体速度不会消除工程纪律，而是将其重构到上游——即规格精度、显式门控和可审计溯源之中。同时给出了团队角色蜕变（工程师、QA、平台、产品）和实用化采用路径（混合估算、分阶段迁移蓝图）。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：规格驱动智能体开发是未来软件交付的主导范式，其本质不是用自动化取代工程纪律，而是将学科纪律前置化——纪律从"写代码时"转移到"写规格时"，通过精确规格、明确门控和可审计的来源追踪来保障自主交付的可靠性与问责制。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [72] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO)，一个为LLM编码代理（Claude Code）提供跨会话持久记忆的系统。PAO在启动时从用户已有的两个异构记忆后端（PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引）并行检索并进行融合，通过文件系统注入将上下文简报交付给代理。论文以经验报告形式记录了2025年12月至2026年3月共四个月的实际部署，涵盖了三代上下文交付机制的演进、失败模式及工程权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理每次启动会话时上下文窗口为空，导致之前工作中积累的知识全部丢失，严重影响了代理在长期、多会话任务中的效率和一致性。现有系统缺乏一种通用的、能够利用用户已有个人数据库来跨会话传递记忆的机制。

Method: PAO的核心方法包括：（1）在代理生成时并行查询两个独立的记忆后端（PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引）；（2）对不同后端采用各自的检索策略并进行结果融合；（3）通过文件系统注入利用宿主代理（Claude Code）的配置自动读取行为来交付上下文简报；（4）管理完整的代理生命周期，包括信任预置、就绪轮询与错误检测、自适应终端文本注入；（5）记录三代上下文交付机制的设计迭代。

Result: 论文作为经验报告呈现，记录了四个月的常规部署期间三代上下文交付机制的演变过程、每种重设计所针对的失败模式，以及连接异构记忆系统而非构建统一系统的工程权衡。具体量化结果在摘要中未有详细披露。

Conclusion: PAO验证了通过编排现有异构记忆基础设施而非重建统一记忆系统来为LLM编码代理提供持久上下文的可行路径。系统在四个月的实际部署中持续演进，表明基于文件系统注入和并行后端检索的记忆编排方案能够在真实生产环境中稳定运行。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [73] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM安全对齐的表面性缺陷——模型仅在生成最后阶段触发拒绝机制，而未真正消除预训练中习得的有害知识。通过分析三个SLM家族在对抗性攻击下的潜在激活轨迹，发现存在一个普适的"意图地平线"（约15-20%层深度），在此处有害意图的表征坍缩为"安全"叙事。基于此提出Latent Intent Verification (LIV)轻量级探测防御方法，在无需重训模型的情况下有效中和零日语义攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的安全对齐机制本质上是表层的——仅依赖生成末期的拒绝机制来阻止有害输出，并未真正从模型内部铲除预训练阶段习得的有害概念知识。这种架构断层使模型容易遭受"语义伪装"攻击：有害意图被包裹在良性的叙事语境（如创意写作）中，从而绕过标准的输入/输出护栏。作者旨在揭示这一漏洞的深层机制，并提出无需重新训练的高效防御方案。

Method: 作者分析了三个不同的SLM家族（Phi-3、Qwen2.5和Gemma-2b）在对抗压力下的潜在激活轨迹，识别出一个普适的"意图地平线"——通常在总层数的15-20%深处，模型对有害意图的独特预训练表征在此坍缩，将查询语境化为"安全"叙事。基于此发现，提出了Latent Intent Verification (LIV)——一种轻量级探测防御方法，利用早期层中仍可检测的"有害特征"来识别伪装攻击，而无需修改模型或重新训练。

Result: 实验表明：晚期层中对伪装攻击的表征在数学上与安全查询不可区分（检测率低于20%），而早期层保留了独特的可检测"有害特征"。在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上，LIV在所有测试架构上的表现优于标准护栏20-50%，有效中和零日语义攻击。

Conclusion: 本研究证明安全对齐的"深度防御"是必要的——仅靠生成末期的拒绝机制不足以抵御语义伪装攻击。利用早期层中残留的有害表征信号，可以在不牺牲模型功能且无需重训的情况下构建高效的安全防御层。这为LLM安全领域提供了新的防御范式：从"末端拦截"转向"源头检测"。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [74] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了多模态在智能体（Agent）框架中的核心作用，从感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动五个功能模块出发，分析了从文本中心智能体到多模态智能体的演进路径，并提出了以模态为中心的智能体分类体系，覆盖机器人、GUI与Web导航、多媒体生成编辑、长视频理解检索等应用领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有大量关于基于LLM的智能体的综述，但在近年来多模态如何塑造智能体能力方面缺乏系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统分析多模态性在智能体框架中的核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）中的影响。

Method: 采用以功能模块为核心的分析视角（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动），追踪从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演进；通过委托（delegated）、晚期融合（late-fusion）和早期融合（early-fusion）三种架构分析模态集成方式；提出以模态为中心的智能体分类法，将架构设计选择与智能体能力关联起来；并对多个应用领域（机器人、GUI与Web导航、多模态内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索）的智能体系统进行全面评估。

Result: 系统梳理了多模态智能体的现有工作，构建了模态中心分类法，分析了不同架构设计下的智能体能力表现，并评估了各应用领域的性能，同时讨论了效率与可扩展性之间的权衡，包括训练和推理成本、延迟和部署约束。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦多模态对智能体设计的影响，识别出当前关键研究空白，并为构建鲁棒、通用的智能系统绘制了未来路线图。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [75] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于树集成（Tree-based Ensembles）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，兼顾预测精度与可解释性。该框架通过编码-聚类-路由-树分类的流程处理文本、音频、视觉三种模态数据，并引入改进的特征重要性指标来增强跨模态概念的可解释性。实验表明，提出的LDT、LDF和LDAB模型在F1-mod上较多模态Transformer提升4.3%，在准确率上较可解释基线IMR提升3.0%，且改进的特征重要性指标在人类标注一致性上显著优于默认方法（IEMOCAP 62.2% vs 43.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高容量多模态模型（如Transformer）虽预测性能强，但其分布式表示和深度非线性导致难以对单个多模态特征赋予有意义的权重，限制了在临床情感监测、教育评估等信任敏感场景中的应用。现有方法无法同时满足竞争性精度与人类可理解的决策解释这一双重目标。

Method: 构建基于树集成框架：1）将每种模态编码为token；2）提取并聚类概念以降维；3）将融合后的模态通过树集成分类器（LDT、LDF、LDAB）进行路由与分类；4）提出一种改进的特征重要性度量，降低二分类任务中负类的影响，从而提升指标/标记检测能力。

Result: 树集成模型在F1-mod上较Multimodal Transformer提升4.3%，在准确率上较可解释基线IMR提升3.0%。改进的多模态特征重要性在人类标注一致性上大幅优于默认特征重要性（IEMOCAP上62.2% vs 43.2%；CMU-MOSI上46.7% vs 32.1%）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明树集成方法能够在保持竞争性预测精度的同时提供显著优于现有方法的人类可理解解释，弥补了Transformer在高性能与可解释性之间的鸿沟，为信任敏感的多模态应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal affect and behaviour classifiers that fuse heterogeneous text, audio, and visual streams must simultaneously achieve competitive accuracy and produce human-understandable explanations of the cues driving their decisions -- a dual objective that current high-capacity models, notably Transformers, only partially address. While Transformers attain strong predictive performance, their distributed representations and deep nonlinearity make it difficult to assign meaningful importance weights to individual multimodal features, limiting their use in trust-sensitive applications such as clinical affect monitoring and educational assessment. We address this gap by developing a framework based on tree-based ensembles that balances accuracy and interpretability. The framework encodes each modality into tokens, extracts and clusters concepts to reduce dimensionality, routes the fused modalities through tree-based ensemble classifiers, and interprets trends using a novel modified feature importance metric.
