These results show that secure edge LLM adaptation should jointly consider hardware behavior, workload regulation, sparse communication, and aggregation robustness.

</details>


### [204] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Constructing Planning Domain Models from Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21186)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Michael Skinner,Prasad Tadepalli,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NSPIN，一个神经符号框架，用于从非结构化临床叙事中归纳概率规划领域模型。该方法利用预训练LLM从原始文本中提取和插补结构化事件序列，归纳PPDDL模型，并通过LLM提出的修订结合经验验证来优化前置条件。在2,660份腹腔镜阑尾切除术记录上评估，模型能泛化到未见笔记，专家评审确认其归纳知识与外科实践高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜阑尾切除术等外科手术是复杂且高风险的过程，但将其工作流形式化用于决策支持仍然困难重重。核心挑战在于：缺乏结构化事件数据以及临床叙述中普遍存在隐含动作，这使得传统符号方法和LLM都难以独立解决概率规划领域模型的归纳问题。

Method: NSPIN框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 使用预训练LLM从非结构化临床文本中提取和插补结构化事件序列；(2) 从提取的序列中归纳PPDDL（概率规划领域定义语言）模型；(3) 利用LLM提出的修订建议对模型前置条件进行细化，并以经验验证作为引导。

Result: 在9位外科医生书写的2,660份腹腔镜阑尾切除术笔记上评估，NSPIN生成的模型能够泛化到未见过的笔记。专家临床评审表明其归纳的知识与外科手术实践大体一致。

Conclusion: NSPIN成功弥合了非结构化临床文本与概率规划模型之间的鸿沟，验证了神经符号方法在医学领域规划模型归纳中的有效性，为外科手术决策支持系统的构建提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Surgical procedures such as laparoscopic appendectomy are complex, high-stakes processes, yet formalizing their workflows for decision support remains a significant challenge. Inducing probabilistic planning domain models in this setting is particularly difficult due to the lack of structured event data and the prevalence of implicit actions in clinical narratives, which neither empirical symbolic methods nor Large Language Models (LLMs) can adequately address on their own. We introduce NSPIN, a neurosymbolic framework for inducing probabilistic planning domain models from unstructured clinical narratives. Our method extracts and imputes structured event sequences from raw text using a pretrained LLM, then induces a PPDDL model and refines its preconditions with LLM-proposed revisions, guided by empirical validation. We evaluate the approach on 2,660 laparoscopic appendectomy notes written by 9 surgeons. NSPIN yields models that generalize to unseen notes, and expert clinical review indicates its induced knowledge is largely consistent with surgical practice.

</details>


### [205] [Tydra: An Efficient Hybrid Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21199)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tydra是一种混合Transformer与状态空间模型（SSM）的表格式上下文学习架构，通过交错注意力层和SSM层，在30个OpenML数据集上将推理时间比TabPFN降低30%，同时保持了大部分预测性能，并优于约十倍大的Hydra模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的表格式基础模型（如TabPFN）预测性能强但计算开销随上下文长度呈二次方增长；而基于SSM的次二次方替代方案（如Hydra）在效率上更优但牺牲了准确性。作者希望在这两者之间找到平衡，兼顾效率与性能。

Method: Tydra采用混合架构，将Transformer的注意力层与SSM层交错排列，从而在保持上下文学习能力的同时降低计算复杂度。通过注意力层捕捉局部/关键依赖，SSM层处理长序列依赖，实现计算效率与表示能力的均衡。

Result: 在30个OpenML基准数据集上，Tydra相比TabPFN降低了30%的推理时间，同时保持了大部分预测性能；相比参数规模约大十倍的Hydra模型，Tydra在预测性能上更优且推理速度更快。

Conclusion: 混合架构是表格式基础模型领域一个有前景的研究方向，能够在不显著牺牲精度的前提下实现显著的计算效率提升。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur quadratic computational cost with context length. On the other hand, subquadratic SSM-based alternatives such as Hydra trade away accuracy for efficiency. To balance both, we introduce Tydra, a hybrid Transformer-State Space Model (SSM) architecture for tabular in-context learning that interleaves attention and SSM layers. Across 30 OpenML datasets, Tydra reduces inference time by 30% relative to TabPFN while retaining much of its predictive performance. Tydra also outperforms an approximately ten-times-larger Hydra model while providing faster inference. The results indicate that hybrid architectures are a promising direction for tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [206] [Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine: Learned Physiological Time-Series Imputation under Realistic Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21207)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Haochen Zhang,Nicholas Konz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAIR（Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine），一种用于生理时间序列插补的两阶段框架，解决了现有方法在生理数据缺失场景中表现不佳的问题——因为生理缺失往往发生在临床极端值处且间隙长度跨越多个数量级。CAIR通过双向GRU插补器生成粗略基线曲线，再由Transformer逐步细化修正三次，并采用信号无关的随机间隔课程训练。在AI-READI连续血糖监测和MIMIC-III动脉血压数据上，CAIR在所有缺失机制下均达到最优精度，且在难度增加时优势扩大（从MCAR下9%到值依赖缺失下19%），并同时保持临床负担指标的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理时间序列插补对临床数据至关重要，但现有方法在该领域表现不佳——简单线性插值甚至优于所有学习型插补器。作者发现原因在于生理缺失的两个特性：缺隙可能发生在信号临床极端而非典型值处，且间隙长度跨度可达数量级差异。这些特性被通用插补器忽略，因此需要专门设计的框架。

Method: CAIR采用两阶段框架：首先用双向GRU插补器生成粗略基线曲线（插补阶段），然后通过Transformer细化器在三次连续迭代中逐步将估计修正到生理学现实（细化阶段）。两者联合训练，使用信号无关的随机间隔课程来逐步增加训练难度。

Result: CAIR在连续血糖监测（AI-READI）和重症监护动脉血压（MIMIC-III）上，按间隙长度和缺失机制（MCAR、MAR、NMAR）分层评估均为最准确。相较最强基线，MCAR下提升9%，值依赖缺失（NMAR）下提升19%。此外，仅低重建误差不足以恢复临床负担指标，而CAIR在精度与临床指标两个维度均最优。

Conclusion: 生理时间序列插补需要专门的设计，不能依赖通用插补方法。采用先插补后细化的两阶段策略结合课程学习，能够同时实现高精度插补与临床负担指标的保持，为临床数据插补提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Imputing physiological time series (arterial blood pressure, blood glucose, etc.) is essential for addressing the missingness that pervades clinical data. Yet modern imputation methods perform poorly in this domain: a recent benchmark found that simple linear interpolation outperformed every learned imputer on real-world clinical signals with realistic gaps. We show that this reflects two properties of physiological missingness that generic imputers ignore: gaps may occur when the signal is clinically extreme rather than typical, and gap lengths can easily span orders of magnitude. To this end, we introduce Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine (CAIR), a two-stage framework for physiological time-series imputation. Our key motivation is to learn a coarse base curve and then repeatedly correct it toward physiological realism, rather than predict a gap in a single pass. Consequently, CAIR couples a bidirectional-GRU interpolator with a Transformer refiner that corrects its own estimate over three successive passes, trained jointly under a broad, signal-agnostic random-gap curriculum. We evaluate imputers stratified by gap length and missingness mechanism (MCAR, MAR, NMAR) rather than by a single average, and CAIR is the most accurate under every mechanism on continuous glucose monitoring (AI-READI) and arterial pressure in intensive care (MIMIC-III). Its margin over the strongest baseline grows with difficulty, from 9% under MCAR to 19% under value-dependent dropout, where generic learned imputers are weakest. We further show low reconstruction error alone does not recover the burden metrics clinicians act on: interpolants matching CAIR's error fail to preserve those metrics, imputers that recover them are far less accurate, and CAIR alone ranks among the best on both axes.

</details>


### [207] [TRACE-C: Rank-Calibrated Relational Anomaly Detection for Multi-Stream Operational Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21251)
*Matthew Faucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE-C 是一种针对对齐多流遥测数据的严格先验秩校准异常检测器，通过三个窗口通道（局部归一化和、高斯copula形式依赖对比、标准化AR(1)创新）的Fisher聚合与秩比较来检测联合异常。在2019年英国电网数据上，其将Storm Atiyah排第一（但归因于局部通道而非copula通道），2020年hold-out数据无窗口被选出（归因于记录规则饱和而非无事件）。论文明确声明p值是选择量而非事件概率、copula形式通道并非真正的copula密度、经验秩计数非覆盖率证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作遥测数据中可以出现所有单流都在正常范围内但联合异常的情形。作者希望构建一个可审计的、严格先验秩校准的检测器，能够检测对齐多流遥测中的联合异常，并透明地披露各通道的贡献与方法的解释局限性。

Method: TRACE-C 使用同一regime下的滚动中位数/MAD残差，馈入三个窗口通道：(1) 最大归一化局部和（局部通道），(2) 基于稳健z残差的高斯copula形式依赖对比（copula形式通道），(3) 最差标准化AR(1)创新（时间通道）。三个通道的秩通过Fisher方法聚合，再与实际观测值与该分布进行秩比较。整个方法严格遵循先验秩校准原则。

Result: 在英格兰六条电网流上评估：2019年1-4月拟合、7-12月开发验证、2020年为预先冻结的hold-out。fused检测器将Storm Atiyah排在2019年测试窗口第一，但通道消融显示该排名来自局部通道而非copula通道（copula单独将Atiyah排第59位）。短时8月9日频率事件被fused检测器排第143位（远低于时间通道单独的第40位），而重建基线将其排第一。2020年无窗口被选中，被解释为记录规则饱和而非平安年份；最高排名的冻结窗口后续被解读为Storm Ellen。

Conclusion: TRACE-C展示了严格先验秩校准联合异常检测的可行性，但论文强调了三个解释性限制：p值是选择量而非事件概率；copula形式通道并非字面意义上的copula密度（未使用概率积分或正态分数变换）；经验秩计数只是诊断指标，并非覆盖率或FDR证明。所有图表均基于已提交的机器可读报告生成，确保可复现性。

Abstract: Operational telemetry can be jointly anomalous while every individual stream stays inside its familiar range. TRACE-C is an auditable strictly-prior rank-calibrated detector for aligned multi-stream telemetry: same-regime rolling median/MAD residuals feed three window channels -- a maximum normalized local sum, a Gaussian copula-form dependence contrast on robust-z residuals, and a worst standardized AR(1) innovation -- whose channel ranks are Fisher-aggregated and ranked against earlier aggregates.
  We evaluate six Great Britain grid streams with a January-April 2019 fit, July-December 2019 development evidence, and a 2020 hold-out frozen before inspection. TRACE-C ranks Storm Atiyah first among 2019 test windows, but a disclosed channel ablation attributes that rank to the local channel, not the copula-form channel: copula-only ranks Atiyah 59th. The short 9 August frequency event is ranked far lower by the fused detector (143) than by the temporal channel alone (40), and reconstruction baselines rank it first. In 2020 no window is selected, which is consistent with record-rule saturation rather than an uneventful year; the highest-ranked frozen window was later interpreted as Storm Ellen.
  Three interpretive limits carry throughout. The resulting p-values are selection quantities, not event probabilities. The copula-form channel is not a literal copula density: the method applies no probability-integral or normal-score transform. Empirical rank counts are diagnostics, not coverage or false-discovery proofs. Every table and figure in this paper is generated from committed machine-readable reports.

</details>


### [208] [ConceptTS: LLM-Guided Concept Bottlenecks for Interpretable Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21277)
*Yichen Jiang,Yueqiao Chen,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConceptTS是一个可解释的多变量时间序列预测框架，利用大语言模型生成可在语义上理解的概念和标签规则，将LLM的领域知识转化为直接监督信号，无需昂贵的人工注释。它通过三个互补瓶颈组织概念（历史上下文、局部预测区间、完整预测范围），共享解码器由预测的概念激活构建预测，使决策过程透明且支持直接的概念级干预。在空气质量数据集上达到与强黑盒基线相当的精度，同时产生语义上有意义的概念激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最先进的多变量时间序列预测器虽然能建模复杂的时间与跨变量依赖关系，但其表示是不透明的，无法解释为何产生特定预测。这种透明度缺失限制了在需要理解并评估预测背后因素的应用场景中的使用。

Method: ConceptTS框架包括：1) 使用LLM提出任务相关概念并生成可执行的标注规则，将LLM领域知识转化为直接监督；2) 将概念组织为三个互补瓶颈，分别描述历史上下文、局部预测区间和完整预测范围；3) 共享解码器结合由预测激活推导的表征来构建预测，使决策过程显式化，支持直接概念级干预。

Result: 在北京多站点空气质量数据集上的实验表明，ConceptTS在预测精度上与强黑盒基线相当，同时产生语义上有意义的概念激活。

Conclusion: ConceptTS表明，可以通过LLM自动生成概念与标注规则，在保持预测精度的同时实现可解释性和可干预性，为时间序列预测的可信应用提供了新路径。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multivariate time-series forecasters can model complex temporal and cross-variable dependencies, yet their opaque representations provide limited insight into why a particular forecast is produced. This lack of transparency restricts their use in settings where practitioners must understand and assess the factors underlying a prediction. We introduce ConceptTS, an interpretable forecasting framework that organizes its predictions around named, human-readable concepts. ConceptTS uses a large language model to propose task-relevant concepts and generate executable labeling rules, translating the language model's domain knowledge into direct supervision without costly manual concept annotation. The proposed concepts are organized into three complementary bottlenecks that describe the historical context, local forecast intervals, and the full forecast horizon. A shared decoder combines representations derived from their predicted activations to construct the forecast, making the model's decision process explicit and supporting direct concept-level interventions. Experiments on the Beijing Multi-Site Air Quality dataset show that ConceptTS achieves accuracy competitive with strong black-box baselines while producing semantically meaningful concept activations.

</details>


### [209] [SPARCL: Spectral Partitioned Analytic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21307)
*James Hartley,Zeropy Surio,Daniel Whitmore,Hannah Clarke,Thomas Reed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARCL，一种基于谱分解的解析持续学习方法，通过将自相关矩阵分解为核心子空间和残差子空间，冻结旧类分类器在核心子空间的成分，仅更新残差块，从而解决解析持续学习中的谱干扰问题，在多个基准上接近强表示匹配方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析持续学习（Analytic Continual Learning）虽以闭式岭回归替代梯度迭代优化，避免了传统的随机梯度覆盖式遗忘，但在旧类上仍存在漂移。传统遗忘叙事无法解释这一现象，作者识别出根本原因是谱干扰：所有任务的联合岭分类器共享逆自相关算子(R+λI)^{-1}，新任务样本若加载到旧主导特征方向，会稀释谱并扰动旧类logits，即使旧标签从未被重新访问。

Method: SPARCL将运行中的自相关矩阵分解为高能量核心子空间和残差补集两部分。核心子空间中的旧类分类器成分被冻结，仅通过递归最小二乘法更新残差块，并可选地引入残差随机投影扩展。该方法产生简单的闭式更新规则，并具有核心子空间贡献的旧类logits可证明不变性保证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R和ImageNet-A上，采用冻结的ViT-B/16协议，SPARCL缩小了经典解析学习器与强表示匹配方法之间的大部分差距，并且与稀疏特征去相关方法（如Fly-CL）具有互补性。

Conclusion: 谱干扰是解析持续学习中旧类漂移的根本原因，通过谱划分冻结核心子空间可以有效缓解该问题。SPARCL提供了一种简单、闭式且具有理论保证的解决方案，可与其他特征去相关方法互补使用。

Abstract: Analytic continual learning has emerged as a strong exemplar-free alternative to gradient-based class-incremental learning because it replaces iterative optimization with closed-form ridge updates. Yet the usual forgetting narrative, centered on stochastic gradient overwriting, does not explain why analytic methods still drift on old classes despite exact recursive solvers. We identify the culprit as spectral interference: the joint ridge classifier for all tasks shares the inverse autocorrelation operator $(R+λI)^{-1}$, so incoming task samples that load onto old dominant eigendirections dilute the spectrum and perturb old-class logits even when old labels are never revisited. Based on this view, we propose SPARCL, a spectral partitioned analytic continual learner that decomposes the running autocorrelation into a high-energy core and a residual complement, freezes old-class classifier components in the core subspace, and updates only the residual block through recursive least squares with an optional residual random-projection expansion. This yields a simple closed-form update with a provable invariance guarantee for the core contribution of old logits. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, ImageNet-R, and ImageNet-A under a frozen ViT-B/16 protocol, SPARCL closes most of the gap from classical analytic learners to strong representation matchers, while remaining complementary to sparse feature-decorrelation approaches such as Fly-CL.

</details>


### [210] [Rethinking Expressivity and Efficiency in Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21308)
*Zeyun Zhong,Joya Chen,Manuel Martin,Frederik Diederichs,Juergen Gall,Juergen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出E²-TTT方法，在标准的chunk-start权重梯度近似下，推导出精确再现逐token循环的chunk-end快权重和动量状态的闭式状态转移，实现了完全并行化的chunk级训练，同时保留逐token更新规则的时间结构，兼顾了表达力与硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时训练（TTT）方法在长上下文处理中面临两难：逐token更新具有高表达力但硬件效率低，而chunk级近似方法硬件高效但丢弃了更新规则的时间结构，从而损失表达力。需要一种方法同时兼顾两者。

Method: 在标准的"在chunk起始权重处取梯度"近似下，推导闭式状态转移公式，精确复现逐token循环中chunk末端的快速权重和动量状态。这使得可以在不丢失时间结构的前提下，将逐token循环转换为完全并行化的chunk级训练。

Result: 训练了最高1.3B参数规模的模型。在语言建模上与先前TTT和混合注意力基线持平，在上下文检索任务上优于基线。在长度外推方面优势显著：标准"大海捞针"密码测试中，在8倍训练上下文长度下仍保持超过90%的准确率。训练吞吐量与高效的chunk方法相匹配。

Conclusion: E²-TTT成功弥合了表达力与效率之间的鸿沟，证明了可以在不牺牲chunk级并行化效率的前提下保留逐token更新规则的时间结构，从而在大幅长度外推和上下文检索任务上取得优异表现。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) enables long-context processing via continuous weight updates during inference, but current methods struggle to balance the expressivity of per-token update dynamics with the hardware efficiency of chunk-wise approximations. We propose E$^2$-TTT (Expressive and Efficient TTT) to bridge this gap. Under the standard approximation of taking gradients at the chunk-start weights, we derive a closed-form state transition that exactly reproduces the chunk-end fast-weight and momentum states of the per-token recurrence. This enables fully parallelized chunk-level training while preserving the temporal structure of the update rule that prior chunk-wise methods discard. We validate E$^2$-TTT by training models up to 1.3B parameters from scratch. It performs on par with previous TTT and hybrid attention baselines in language modeling while outperforming them on in-context retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced in length extrapolation: on the standard ``Needle in a Haystack'' passkey test, it retains over 90% accuracy at $8\times$ the training context length. Meanwhile, E$^2$-TTT can match the training throughput of efficient chunk-wise methods, demonstrating that it effectively reconciles expressivity with efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/zeyun-zhong/E2-TTT.

</details>


### [211] [Time-Aware Tranformer-Based Prediction Model for AECOPD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21324)
*Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Dongyang Wang,Jiacun Wang,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Time-Aware Transformer的慢阻肺急性加重（AECOPD）预测模型，利用家用呼吸机的呼吸数据进行时序预测，以解决传统机器学习模型依赖临床和实验室数据导致的时间延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD症状变化迅速，急需时间敏感的预测模型。然而现有基于机器学习的AECOPD研究多使用临床和实验室数据，这些数据获取存在不可避免的延迟，难以满足及时检测的需求。

Method: 提出Time-Aware Transformer架构，通过时间感知机制捕捉呼吸机数据中症状及其时间演变过程，生成有意义的患者表征用于AECOPD预测。该方法面向家庭监测场景，仅使用日常使用的无创呼吸机产生的呼吸数据。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于Time-Aware Transformer的方法在多个分类任务中优于传统方法，展现了提升AECOPD预测准确率的潜力。

Conclusion: Time-Aware Transformer模型利用家用呼吸机数据进行时序建模，能够在减少延迟的同时有效提升AECOPD预测准确性，具有临床应用前景。

Abstract: The rapid symptom change of Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) makes it critical to have time-sensitive prediction models. However, most current machine learning models studying AECOPD use clinical and laboratory data, which will inevitably cause latency. To ensure timely detection of AECOPD and minimize latency, this paper focuses on home monitoring scenarios where only respiratory data from daily-use ventilators is available. We introduce a Time-Aware transformer-based AECOPD prediction model, which generates meaningful patient representations using the Time-Aware transformer to capture the symptoms and their temporal progression in ventilator data. Our experimental results demonstrate that our Time-Aware transformer-based approach outperforms traditional methods in multiple classification tasks, highlighting its potential to enhance AECOPD prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [212] [Across-Design Uncertainty in Short Pricing Panels: Evidence from Simulated Price Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21334)
*Pedro Cadahia Delgado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏定价面板数据中，估计误差方差的主要来源并非条件于已实现价格轨迹的抽样不确定性，而是跨设计（不同轨迹）的变化。基线模拟中后者占梯度提升估计误差方差的97.6%，并提出了经验关系、跨区域/单位聚合效应及Paule-Mandel方差分量校准等三项结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短观测期定价面板可能包含大量观测值，但价格变动次数极少。这种稀疏定价机制下，标准推断程序（如面板内重抽样）可能严重低估估计误差的真实不确定性，从而产生误导性的置信区间和统计推断。

Method: 采用校准到稀疏定价机制的综合数据生成过程（DGP）。将估计误差的方差分解为两个部分：条件于给定价格轨迹的不确定性，以及同一定价过程生成的不同轨迹之间估计误差的变化。使用梯度提升等机器学习规格进行基线模拟，并利用Paule-Mandel方差分量估计跨单位方差。

Result: （1）跨设计离散度满足经验关系sigma_hat≈0.182 V^(-0.271)，其中V=价格变动次数×变动幅度的平方；（2）共享同一价格路径的额外区域仅降低结果噪声，不产生独立价格轨迹；而独立设计误差的单位平均以标准平方根速率降低离散度；（3）Paule-Mandel方差分量将同质模拟中的经验覆盖率从0.469提升到0.931。

Conclusion: 核心含义是应从依赖固定被动面板转向设计能产生独立识别变动的数据生成过程。面板内重抽样无法识别跨设计方差分量，因此需要更重视跨独立定价单位的设计来获取可靠的推断。

Abstract: Short observational pricing panels can contain many observations while offering only a small number of distinct price movements. This paper studies the inferential consequences of that distinction in a synthetic data-generating process calibrated to a sparse pricing regime. We separate uncertainty conditional on a realised price trajectory from variation in estimation error across alternative trajectories generated by the same pricing process. In the baseline simulations, the latter component accounts for 97.6% of the variance of estimation error for the gradient-boosted specification. Within-panel resampling procedures use the information of one realised trajectory and do not identify this across-design component.
  Three results organise the analysis. First, across-design dispersion is well described by the empirical relation sigma_hat approx 0.182 V^(-0.271), where V equals moves times magnitude squared. Second, adding regions sharing a common price path reduces outcome noise but does not create independent price trajectories; conversely, averaging across units with independent design-specific errors reduces dispersion at the standard square root rate. Third, a Paule-Mandel variance component estimated across independently priced units substantially increases empirical coverage in homogeneous simulations, from 0.469 to 0.931. The broader implication is a shift toward designing data-generating processes that create independent identifying variation rather than relying solely on fixed passive panels.

</details>


### [213] [Asymmetric Capacity Allocation in Self-Refinement Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21345)
*Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Salar Hashemitaheri,Cassie Huang,Yuangang Li,Hyunwoo Oh,Paul Dourish,Tony Givargis,Mohsen Imani,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统地按阶段（生成、批判、修订）分析自我精炼流程中模型规模的影响，覆盖5个基准、6种Qwen3和4种Gemma 3模型大小，发现生成器和修订器的规模对性能至关重要（过小的修订器甚至会损害性能），而批判器的规模对性能极不敏感，仅需小批判器即可超越无批判的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我精炼（生成-批判-修订）是LLM生成和多智能体系统中的核心范式，但现有方法通常将模型大小视为实现细节而非研究对象，导致资源浪费，缺乏对不同阶段所需模型能力的系统性理解。

Method: 在5个不同领域的基准上，使用6种规模的Qwen3和4种规模的Gemma 3模型，对自我精炼流水线的三个关键阶段（生成器、批判器、修订器）进行逐阶段的模型规模影响研究。

Result: 更大的生成器和修订器通常提升流水线整体性能，但过小的修订器会损害性能；批判器的规模对性能高度不敏感，而加入即使很小的批判器也始终优于完全省略批判阶段。

Conclusion: 模型容量不应在自我精炼流水线中均匀分配，不同阶段具有不同的规模缩放特性，这为设计更计算高效的多阶段语言模型系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Self-refinement, typically structured as generation, critique, and revision, is a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLM generation and serves as a core mechanism in many LLM agents. While the three stages involve different cognitive demands, most existing approaches conveniently treat the model size as an implementation detail rather than a subject of study, which may lead to a waste of resources. Little work has systematically examined how model size affects each stage or whether effective self-refinement requires equally capable models for generation, critique, and revision. We present the first stage-wise model size study of the self-refinement pipeline on 5 benchmarks from different domains using 6 model sizes of Qwen3 and 4 model sizes of Gemma 3. We conclude that larger generators and refiners generally improve the pipeline, whereas an undersized refiner can even harm performance. Second, performance is highly insensitive to the size of the critic, although including even a small critic consistently outperforms omitting critique altogether. Our findings demonstrate that model capacity should not be allocated uniformly across self-refinement pipelines. Instead, different stages exhibit distinct size scaling characteristics, providing practical guidance for designing more computationally efficient multi-stage language model systems.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Strong growth and Goldstein subgradients in piecewise smooth optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20642)
*Adrian S. Lewis,Fahaar M. Pirani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种针对Lipschitz函数在局部极小值点附近的新增长条件，该条件源自Goldstein次梯度算法在实际中的行为。对于分段二次连续可微的目标函数，证明了在强凸情形下该增长条件成立；在非凸情形下，二次增长加上活动梯度的正则性性质即可保证该条件成立，并通过数值实验验证了假设的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldstein次梯度类算法在实际运行中往往表现出近似线性收敛，但现有理论难以解释这一现象。作者希望从新的增长条件出发，为这类算法的实际收敛行为提供理论支撑。

Method: 针对分段C²连续可微的目标函数，分别考虑强凸和非凸两种情形：在强凸条件下直接证明新增长条件成立；在非凸情形下，结合二次增长条件与活动梯度的正则性性质来证明新增长条件成立。同时设计数值实验验证各假设条件的必要性。

Result: 在强凸情形下新增长条件必然成立；在非凸情形下，二次增长与活动梯度正则性共同保证了新增长条件的成立；数值实验表明每个假设条件都是不可缺少的。

Conclusion: 该工作为新增长条件提供了充分的函数类刻画，揭示了Goldstein次梯度算法近似线性收敛背后的函数结构条件，对进一步理解非光滑优化的收敛性理论有重要意义。

Abstract: We explore a new growth condition for Lipschitz functions around local minimizers, recently proposed in the context of Goldstein-subgradient-based algorithms and their behavior in practice. On generic examples, these algorithms are often observed to converge approximately linearly. We focus on objectives that are piecewise twice continuously differentiable. In that case, the new growth condition holds when the objective is, in addition, strongly convex. In the nonconvex case, we prove that quadratic growth in conjunction with a regularity property for the active gradients suffices. Computational experiments illustrate the importance of the assumptions.

</details>


### [215] [ResiliFlow: An Open Transport World Model for Infrastructure Perception and Disaster Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20709)
*Junxiang Xu,Vinayak Dixit,S. Travis Waller,Divya Jayakumar Nair,Qianwen,Guo,Sisi Jian,Xiao Wen,Ashutosh Ashutosh,Sunhyung Yoo,Julius Secadiningrat,Jingni Guo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ResiliFlow是一个面向交通运输基础设施韧性、响应与恢复的开源世界模型平台，通过连接两个工作区（灾害运输韧性分析与AI基础设施感知决策支持），共享八步循环流程，将分散的数据准备、网络建模、仿真、图像检测和报告整合为一个可检查、可复用、由问题驱动的统一系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通运输韧性研究常常分散在独立的数据预处理脚本、网络模型、仿真工具、图像检查系统和报告中，这种碎片化使得从观测到经过测试和可审查的决策这一过程变得困难。作者旨在解决这一集成性与可审查性不足的问题。

Method: ResiliFlow平台由两个工作区组成：(1) 灾害运输韧性分析提供六个地图中心功能（关键道路/区域识别、恢复优先排序、中断绕行、韧性测试和情景模拟）；(2) AI运输基础设施感知与决策支持组织街景和卫星证据，检测道路、人行道和路缘的可见状况。平台共享八步循环（感知、预测、模型开发、验证、执行、决策、反馈和记忆），并配有研究验证模块记录假设与检查，以及本地助手和可选多提供商大语言模型Copilot将用户问题转化为受限的可执行工具调用。

Result: 在八个可见状况类别上实现了准确的识别结果，并通过紧凑的误差分析展示了困难案例如何指导持续学习。文中记录了平台架构、代表性数学模型、界面证据和计算机视觉学习结果。

Conclusion: ResiliFlow展示了运输模型可以成为一个可检查、可复用、由问题驱动的系统，而非孤立分析的集合。随附发布旨在支持研究协作、公众审查和在机构审查下的扩展。

Abstract: Transport resilience work is often split across separate data preparation scripts, network models, simulation tools, image inspection systems and reports. This fragmentation makes it difficult to move from an observation to a tested and reviewable decision. We introduce ResiliFlow, an open transport world model concept and an implemented platform for infrastructure resilience, response and recovery. The platform connects two workspaces. Disaster Transport Resilience Analysis provides six map-centred functions for critical-road and critical-area identification, recovery prioritisation, disruption routing, resilience testing and scenario simulation. AI-based Transport Infrastructure Perception and Decision Support organises street-level and satellite evidence, detects visible road, footpath and kerb conditions, and prepares these observations for human-reviewed intervention planning. Both workspaces share an eight-step cycle of perception, prediction, model development, verification, execution, decision, feedback and memory. Research Validation records assumptions and checks, while a local Assistant and an optional multi-provider large language model Copilot translate user questions into bounded calls to executable tools. We document the platform architecture, representative mathematical models, interface evidence and computer-vision learning results. Examples show accurate recognition across eight visible-condition classes, while compact error analysis demonstrates how difficult cases guide continued learning. ResiliFlow shows how transport models can become an inspectable, reusable and question-led system rather than a collection of disconnected analyses. The accompanying release is intended to support research collaboration, public scrutiny and extension under institutional review.

</details>


### [216] [Improving Stability and Economic Operation in Transmission Systems through Volt/VAR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20747)
*Shuaicheng Tong,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于同伦连续方法的离散控制无功电压优化（VVO）方法，用于协调有载调压变压器（OLTCs）和电容器组（CBs）等开关设备，在AC潮流可行性的约束下快速恢复电压和VAR水平。在IEEE、PEGASE和RTE系统上的实验表明，该方法能在实际运行时间内产生AC可行设定点，同时降低电压偏差、无功调度和发电成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输电系统运营商在将市场出清的直流调度结果通过交流潮流（ACPF）求解转化为实际运行设定点时，仍可能出现电压越限和无功功率越限的问题。维护安全的电压剖面和充足的无功支撑需要能够在运行中快速实施的纠正性决策，因此需要一种快速的离散控制VVO方法。

Method: 提出一种基于同伦（homotopy）的连续方法来解决离散控制的Volt/VAR优化（VVO）问题，协调OLTC和电容器组等开关型设备，在满足AC物理约束的前提下实现电压和VAR的优化调节。

Result: 在IEEE、PEGASE和RTE系统上的实验表明，所提出的VVO能在实际运行时间内产生AC可行的设定点，同时降低电压偏差、VAR调度量和发电成本。此外，使用ACPF修正后的DC调度作为输入时，VVO能达到与使用AC可行调度作为输入时相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该VVO方法可以自然地集成到现有输电调度实践中，用于增强电网运行的安全性和可靠性，为输电系统运营商提供一种实用的电压和无功优化纠正工具。

Abstract: Transmission system operators often reconcile market-cleared DC dispatches with AC physics through power-flow solves (ACPF), yet the resulting setpoints can still violate voltage (Volt) and reactive-power (VAR) limits. Maintaining secure voltage profiles and adequate VAR support therefore requires fast corrective decisions that are implementable in operation. This paper presents a novel homotopy-based continuation method for discrete-control Volt/VAR Optimization (VVO) that coordinates switchable devices, such as on-load tap-changing transformers (OLTCs) and capacitor banks (CBs). Experiments on IEEE, PEGASE, and RTE systems show that the proposed VVO produces AC-feasible setpoints within practical runtime, while reducing voltage deviation, VAR dispatch, and generation cost. VVO also achieves comparable performance using ACPF-adjusted DC dispatches as inputs relative to AC-feasible dispatches, indicating that it can be integrated naturally into existing transmission dispatch practices to strengthen grid operation.

</details>


### [217] [Time-Inconsistent MDPs with Entropy Regularization: Equilibrium Existence and Policy Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20811)
*Fengyuan Cao,Zhenhua Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究无限时域、状态空间可数无穷且奖励函数无界的时不一致马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过熵正则化和加权泛函分析方法，利用Schauder-Tychonoff不动点定理建立正则化均衡的存在性，并通过紧性、Gibbs策略集中性和一致可积性论证，取熵权重趋于零的极限，得到原始非正则化问题的松弛均衡。进一步，提出策略迭代算法（PIA），在加权折扣结构下证明其指数收敛性和正则化均衡的唯一性，并给出迭代策略构成原始问题加权ε-均衡的显式遗憾估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时不一致的马尔可夫决策过程（时间不一致性来源广泛，奖励显式依赖于初始时间和初始状态）在无限时域、可数无穷状态空间和非有界奖励函数的一般框架下，缺乏系统性的均衡存在性理论和有效的数值算法。现有方法通常假设奖励有界或状态空间有限，无法覆盖更一般的实际应用场景。

Method: 1) 采用熵正则化方法，通过正则化参数λ将原问题转化为正则化均衡问题，利用不动点算子刻画正则化均衡。2) 引入两个具有不同作用的权重函数——一个控制奖励和值函数的增长，另一个定义环境的加权空间——在乘积拓扑下构造紧不变量集，应用Schauder-Tychonoff不动点定理证明正则化均衡的存在性。3) 令λ→0+，通过紧性论证、Gibbs策略浓度分析和一致可积性论证，证明子序列极限为原始非正则化问题的松弛均衡。4) 提出策略迭代算法（PIA），在加权折扣结构下证明其指数收敛性及正则化均衡的唯一性，并结合软最大近似界推导原始问题的加权ε-均衡及显式遗憾估计。

Result: 建立了正则化均衡的存在性定理，并且紧不变量集对λ∈(0,1]可一致选取；证明了当λ→0+时，正则化均衡的子序列极限为原始问题的松弛均衡。对于策略迭代算法，在充分强的折扣条件下建立了指数收敛速度和正则化均衡的唯一性，并给出了量化遗憾估计。此外，提供了数值示例展示强折扣下PIA的收敛性，以及弱折扣下PIA失效的反例。

Conclusion: 本文在非常一般的框架下（无限时域、可数无穷状态空间、无界奖励、时间不一致性）建立了时不一致MDP松弛均衡的存在性理论，并提出了具有收敛保证的策略迭代算法。所得结果推广了已有文献中对时不一致MDP的分析，为更广泛的实际应用提供了理论基础和数值工具。

Abstract: We study infinite-horizon time-inconsistent Markov decision processes with a countably infinite state space and unbounded reward functions. The reward is allowed to depend explicitly on the initial time and initial state, thereby accommodating general sources of time inconsistency. We seek relaxed feedback equilibria, and our approach is based on entropy regularization and weighted functional analytic methods. With entropy regularization, we characterize a regular relaxed equilibrium through a fixed-point operator. By introducing two weight functions with distinct roles, one controlling the growth of rewards and values and the other defining the ambient weighted space, we construct a compact invariant set under a product topology and apply the Schauder-Tychonoff fixed-point theorem to establish existence of regularized equilibria. Importantly, the invariant set can be chosen uniformly for small entropy weight $λ\in(0,1]$. We then let $λ\to0+$ and show, through compactness, concentration of Gibbs policies, and uniform-integrability arguments, that a subsequential limit is a relaxed equilibrium of the original unregularized problem.
  We further study a policy iteration algorithm (PIA) for the entropy-regularized equilibrium problem. Under a weighted-discounting structure and sufficiently strong discounting, we establish exponential convergence and uniqueness of the regularized equilibrium in a suitable weighted Banach space. Combining the policy-iteration error with a quantitative soft-max approximation bound, we show that the iterated policies constitute weighted $\varepsilon$-equilibria for the original unregularized problem and derive an explicit regret estimate. A numerical example illustrating the convergence of PIA under strong discounting and a counterexample demonstrating its failure under weak discounting are also provided.

</details>


### [218] [Failure of Whole-Sequence Convergence in Variable-Smoothing Full-Splitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20859)
*Min Tao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了平滑型全分裂近次梯度方法（S-FSPS）在结构化非凸非光滑分数规划问题中的整序列收敛性。现有理论仅建立了子序列收敛到精确极限提升驻点的结果，本文通过构造两个反例，证明即使每个聚点都是精确极限提升驻点，生成的整序列也不需要收敛，从而否定了整序列收敛性的猜想。这是首个显式构造表明"消失的非可和平滑加上精确极限驻点性并不蕴含整序列收敛"的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boţ、Li和Tao在2025年提出的S-FSPS方法（Algorithm 4.1）用于求解结构化非凸非光滑分数规划，但其现有收敛理论仅证明了存在收敛到精确极限提升驻点的子序列，整序列是否必然收敛这一问题尚未解决。回答这一问题对于理解该算法的理论完备性和实际行为具有重要意义。

Method: 作者通过构造两个可容许的（admissible）实例来证明整序列不收敛。第一个构造中线性算子A非零且每个生成的对偶迭代非零；第二个构造中可行集全维、A行满秩且f∘K和g∘A在可行集上都非常数。两种构造的原始序列的聚点集均为{1}×S¹且轨迹无限长，但每个聚点都是精确极限提升驻点。第一个构造还被用于生成变量平滑单循环全分裂方法在非凸非光滑复合优化中的不收敛反例。

Result: 构造了两个满足所有算法前提条件的实例，其聚类集为{1}×S¹并具有无限长度轨迹，尽管所有聚点都是精确极限提升驻点，整序列并不收敛。第一个构造还提供了变量平滑单循环全分裂方法在非凸非光滑复合优化中的非收敛示例。这是首次显式构造展示消失的非可和平滑不蕴含整序列收敛性。

Conclusion: 平滑型全分裂近次梯度方法在结构化非凸非光滑分数规划中不具备整序列收敛性，即使所有聚点均为精确极限提升驻点且平滑参数以非可和方式趋于零。这一发现填补了该算法收敛理论的空白，并为变量平滑全分裂方案的理论局限性提供了确凿证据。

Abstract: We investigate whole-sequence convergence of the smoothing-based full-splitting proximal subgradient method (S-FSPS) for structured nonconvex and nonsmooth fractional programs, introduced as Algorithm~4.1 by Boţ, Li, and Tao (\emph{SIAM J. Optim.}, 35(4):2623--2653, 2025). The existing convergence theory establishes only the existence of subsequences converging to exact limiting lifted stationary points, leaving open whether the entire generated sequence must converge. We answer this question in the negative by constructing two admissible instances whose primal sequences have cluster set \(\{1\}\times\mathbb S^1\) and infinite-length trajectories, although every cluster point is an exact limiting lifted stationary point. In the first construction, the linear operator \(A\) is nonzero and every generated dual iterate is nonzero. In the second, the feasible set is full-dimensional, \(A\) has full row rank, and both \(f\circ K\) and \(g\circ A\) are nonconstant on the feasible set. The first construction also yields a nonconvergent example for the corresponding variable-smoothing, single-loop, full-splitting method for nonconvex and nonsmooth composite optimization, although a subsequence converges to an exact limiting stationary point. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first explicit construction showing that vanishing nonsummable smoothing, even together with exact limiting stationarity, does not imply whole-sequence convergence for variable-smoothing full-splitting schemes.

</details>


### [219] [Maximum Covering Network Design on Graphs with Low Connectivity: Dynamic Programming and Block-Cut Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20894)
*Felix Rauh,Jannik Matuschke,Hande Yaman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究最大覆盖网络设计问题（MCNDP），即在设施选址和网络升级之间共享单一预算，以最大化目标出行距离内覆盖的人口。作者提出一种精确动态规划框架，利用道路网络低连通性的特点（存在大量割点），在树上实现自包含的快速递归，在一般低连通网络上通过割点分解并嵌入MILP求解子问题。在306个测试案例中，该框架在80%以上的实例中匹配或优于直接求解，且能在单次运行中生成完整的覆盖率-预算曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统选址模型将网络视为固定的、预算视为给定的，但在规划可达性公共服务时，设施选址与网络升级（如修复洪涝频发道路）往往共享预算且相互耦合。现有方法无法有效解决这种联合优化问题，而且规划者需要的是覆盖率随预算变化的完整曲线而非单一方案。

Method: 提出一种精确动态规划框架：(1) 利用道路网络低连通性（大量割点）进行分解；(2) 在树上，状态减少为少数简单的设施和升级选择，无需求解器即可快速计算，运行时间可预测；(3) 在一般低连通网络上，于割点处分解问题并嵌入给定的MILP公式求解子问题，通过界面处的覆盖条件协调各子问题；(4) 单次运行即可评估所有预算水平，得到完整覆盖率-预算曲线。

Result: 在树上，对于较大预算和出行距离，该框架优于直接求解MILP；在一般低连通网络上，306个测试案例中超过80%的实例框架匹配或优于直接求解；单次运行即可生成完整覆盖率-预算曲线，而直接求解需要将时间分配到各个预算上。

Conclusion: 该动态规划框架为最大覆盖网络设计问题提供了一种高效的精确求解方法，尤其适用于现实道路网络这类低连通图结构。它将MILP嵌入分解框架中作为黑盒使用，既保持求解精度又显著提升效率，并免费生成完整预算-覆盖率曲线，为规划者提供了实用的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Planning accessible public services such as health care, emergency response, and schools often requires not only choosing where to open facilities but also improving the network that connects people to them, for example upgrading flood-prone roads in vulnerable regions. Most location models, however, take the network as fixed and the budget as given. We study the Maximum Covering Network Design Problem, in which a single budget is shared between opening facilities and upgrading weak links to maximize the population within a target travel distance of an open facility. The problem is hard even on the simplest networks, and planners usually want to see how coverage grows with the budget, not a single plan.
  We develop an exact dynamic-programming framework that exploits a property common to real road networks: their low connectivity, with many cut points whose removal disconnects the network. On trees, the recursion is self-contained: each state reduces to a few simple facility and upgrade choices that are fast to compute without a solver, giving predictable running times; for larger budgets and travel distances it outperforms solving the MILP formulation directly. On general low-connectivity networks, the framework decomposes the problem at the cut points and embeds a given MILP formulation to solve the resulting pieces, coordinating them through coverage conditions at the interfaces. This lets us compare a formulation on its own against the same formulation inside the framework: across 306 test cases the framework matches or outperforms direct solving on more than 80% of instances. Because it evaluates all budget levels in a single run, it also yields the full coverage-versus-budget curve at no extra cost, whereas direct solving must split its time across individual budgets.

</details>


### [220] [Bridging Semantics and Behavior: An Agent-Based Evolutionary Model of Topic Competition with BERTopic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20996)
*Blekanov I.,Gubar E.,Fan X*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个语言无关的计算框架，将BERTopic语义主题建模与进化博弈论和基于智能体的仿真相结合，在NetLogo中模拟用户通过局部模仿和噪声容忍选择来更新话题偏好，从而复现社交媒体上的话题扩散趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上信息传播与舆论演化是计算社会科学的核⼼挑战，但传统模型往往将内容与行为分离，无法捕捉个体认知与社会互动如何联合驱动大规模话语。需要一个能桥接语义理解与行为仿真的统一框架。

Method: 使用BERTopic从大规模微博数据中提取连贯话题；基于点赞、评论和转发构建统一参与度指标；在NetLogo中构建基于智能体的仿真，通过局部模仿和基于收益比较及行为噪声的偏好更新机制来模拟用户的有限理性决策。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的测试表明，该模型能成功复现观察到的话题扩散趋势，并揭示社会学习频率和收益敏感度如何塑造公众注意力；微观层面的模仿和噪声容忍选择机制可涌现出宏观层面的模式。

Conclusion: 该工作为人机交互领域贡献了一种将语义分析与行为仿真相链接的方法，揭示了在线集体行为的认知与社会基础，并对虚假信息韧性、舆论预测和自适应内容策展具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Understanding how information spreads and opinions evolve on social media is a core challenge in computational social science. Users constantly face competing topics, yet their decisions to engage are shaped by social learning, perceived utility, and cognitive limits. Traditional models often separate content from behavior, missing how individual cognition and social interaction jointly drive large-scale discourse.
  We propose a language-independent computational framework that bridges semantic topic modeling with evolutionary game theory and agent-based simulation. Using BERTopic, we extract coherent topics from a large Weibo dataset. We then quantify engagement through a unified metric based on likes, comments, and reposts. In NetLogo, agents update topic preferences via local imitation, guided by payoff comparisons and behavioral noise---mirroring real-world bounded rationality.
  Tests on two real-world datasets show that our model successfully replicates observed topic diffusion trends. It highlights how social learning frequency and payoff sensitivity shape public attention. Our results demonstrate that micro-level mechanisms---imitation and noise-tolerant choice---can produce emergent macro-level patterns. This work contributes to human-computer interaction by linking semantic analysis with behavioral simulation, offering insights into the cognitive and social foundations of online collective behavior. The framework also has practical potential for misinformation resilience, opinion forecasting, and adaptive content curation.

</details>


### [221] [Optimal Sensor Placement for Output Estimation Using an Artificial Bee Colony Algorithm with Pre-filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21042)
*R. P. P. F. Goetz,Y. Dwaraga,N. van de Wouw,T. Oomen,M. M. J. van de Wal,B. Sharif,H. J. Zwart*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于新型二进制人工蜂群（NBABC）算法与Gramian预滤波器相结合的方法，用于解决3D热弹性系统中卡尔曼滤波估计性能最大化的传感器布置问题（NP-hard），显著降低了计算复杂度并实现了快速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器布置问题在数学上是NP-hard的优化问题，且可行解集随候选位置和传感器数量呈组合爆炸式增长，使得在大规模3D热弹性系统中找到最优布置方案变得极其困难。

Method: 将3D热弹性系统建模为离散时间线性随机模型，采用新型二进制人工蜂群（NBABC）算法进行求解，并引入基于Gramian的预滤波器来减少候选解的搜索空间，从而降低计算复杂度。

Result: 实验结果表明所提出的方法在求解效率和收敛速度方面均表现出明显优势。

Conclusion: NBABC算法结合Gramian预滤波器是解决3D热弹性系统传感器布置问题的有效方案，能够在保证估计性能的前提下显著降低计算复杂度。

Abstract: Sensor placement for maximizing the estimation performance of the Kalman filter is an NP-hard optimization problem. Furthermore, its feasible set grows combinatorially with the candidate locations and the number of sensors. In this paper, we study this sensor placement problem for a 3D thermoelastic system modelled as a discrete-time linear stochastic model. We use the Novel Binary Artificial Bee Colony (NBABC) algorithm with a Gramian-based pre-filter to reduce the computational complexity. Our results show the efficiency and the fast convergence of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [222] [Anchored Regularized Direct Least Squares (ARDLS): Integrating Established Prioritization Operators for Priority Elicitation in the Analytic Hierarchy Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21187)
*Kevin Kam Fung Yuen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种锚定正则化直接最小二乘法（ARDLS）优化模型，通过在正则化惩罚中引入已建立的优先级算子（如归一化技术、特征向量法、奇异值分解、余弦最大化及伪逆Gram矩阵）作为理论锚点，打破了DLS方法的数学对称性，确保收敛到唯一全局最小值，从而解决了DLS方法在高度不一致性（如循环矛盾）下存在多个全局最小值、导致优先级排序不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次分析法（AHP）中的成对互反矩阵是决策建模的基础。直接最小二乘法（DLS）虽能直观推导优先级向量且无需复杂变换，但其优化问题是非凸的，在高不一致水平（如循环矛盾）下会产生多个不同的全局最小值，导致优先级排序不稳定且严重依赖初始猜测。这种结构性缺陷需要被克服。

Method: 提出ARDLS优化模型，将归一化技术、特征向量法、奇异值分解、余弦最大化和伪逆Gram矩阵（加权最小二乘的闭式解）等具有唯一解的优先级算子作为正则化惩罚中的理论锚点，通过系统性地打破数学对称性，倾斜优化地形以保证收敛到单个唯一全局最小值。

Result: 全面的数值实验和模拟验证表明，ARDLS框架能够在保证严格数学唯一性的同时，有效降低各已建立优先级算子之间的均方根误差（RMSE）。

Conclusion: 所提出的ARDLS方法有望成为AHP在众多应用领域中的理想替代方案，因为它同时解决了DLS的多解性和不稳定性问题，并保持了与其他成熟优先级算子的一致性。

Abstract: Pairwise reciprocal matrices are fundamental to the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP), a decision-making model. While the Direct Least Squares (DLS) method provides an intuitive mechanism for deriving priority vectors without complex transformations, the DLS provides multiple solutions. Under high levels of inconsistency, such as cyclic contradictions, this non-convexity yields multiple distinct global minima, resulting in unstable priority rankings that critically depend on initial algorithmic guesses. To overcome this structural deficiency, this paper introduces the Anchored Regularized Direct Least Squares (ARDLS) optimization model. ARDLS integrates uniquely determined established prioritization operators, such as normalization techniques, the Eigenvector method, Singular Value Decomposition, Cosine Maximization, and the Pseudo-Inverse Gram Matrix (the closed-form solution of Weighted Least Squares), as theoretical anchors within a regularization penalty. This integration systematically breaks mathematical symmetries, tilting the optimization landscape to guarantee convergence upon a single, unique global minimum. Comprehensive numerical experiments and simulations validate that the ARDLS framework successfully reduces root mean square error among established priority operators, while guaranteeing strict mathematical uniqueness. The proposed ARDLS may be the ideal alternative for the AHP applied to many application domains.

</details>


### [223] [Primal Acceleration of Newton's Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21359)
*Nikita Doikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种新的直接加速牛顿法，用于最小化具有Lipschitz连续Hessian的凸函数。该算法仅使用原始变量，每次迭代只需一次线性求解，以简单的预定参数选择达到O(1/k^3)的全局收敛率（函数残差意义下）。这是首个针对此类问题在仅依赖单次线性系统求解（无需三次正则化等非线性辅助子问题、非线性参数搜索或对偶外推校正）情况下达到该速率的二阶方法。方法可无Hessian实现，使用非精确线性求解器仍保持快速全局收敛率，并可扩展到Bregman散度下的任意几何以及复合优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高阶优化方法中，加速牛顿法（如三次正则化方法）虽能达到O(1/k^3)的最优收敛率，但每次迭代需要求解昂贵的非线性正则化子问题；而传统牛顿法仅有O(1/k^2)的全局收敛率。作者旨在设计一种既能达到O(1/k^3)加速率、又只需单次线性求解（每次迭代代价低）的新方法，弥补现有方法在计算复杂度与收敛速度之间的缺口。

Method: 提出直接加速牛顿法（DAN方法），通过巧妙的参数设计和动量加速机制，仅在原始变量空间构造迭代格式。每次迭代仅需一次线性系统求解（即Hessian-vector乘积相关的线性方程），无需求解三次正则化等非线性子问题，也不依赖对偶修正。参数以预定闭式公式给出，且允许使用非精确线性求解器（Hessian-free实现）。方法进一步通过Bregman散度推广到非欧几里得几何，并扩展到复合（平滑+非平滑）优化问题。

Result: 新算法对具有Lipschitz连续Hessian的凸函数达到O(1/k^3)的全局函数残差收敛率，为同类问题中首个仅需单次线性求解即可达到该速率的方法。数值结果显示该方法在计算代价与收敛速度上优于已有二阶方法；非精确求解与Bregman扩展等变体同样保持理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的直接加速牛顿法以最低每次迭代计算代价（单次线性求解）达到二阶加速最优收敛率O(1/k^3)，在理论上填补了该问题类中收敛率与计算复杂度的空白。方法具有简洁的实现形式、预定的参数选择、可扩展到非欧几何域与复合优化问题，为大规模优化提供了高效且理论上严谨的新工具。

Abstract: We develop a new direct accelerated Newton method for minimizing convex functions with Lipschitz continuous Hessian. The algorithm uses only primal variables and performs just one linear solve per iteration. With a simple predetermined choice of parameters, it achieves the global convergence rate of $O(1/k^3)$ in terms of the functional residual. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first second-order method for this problem class attaining this rate while relying solely on one linear system solve per iteration (without solving auxiliary nonlinear regularized subproblems, such as cubic regularization, performing nonlinear parameter searches, or using dual extragradient corrections). Our method can be implemented in a Hessian-free way, using an inexact linear system solver, while preserving the fast global rate. We further extend our construction to arbitrary geometry through Bregman divergence, and to composite optimization problems.

</details>
